"
I believe in the Free Worlds Coalition. I believe in its mission. I believe we will succeed. The Empire will be defeated, and freedom will return to this stellar realm. I will not stop until we win, and then I will fight to make sure nothing like the Dominion ever returns.
"
―Mira Venn
Mira Kast Venn Corbin
was a
Flux-sensitive
human
Alderaanian
female
politician
,
Keeper
, and
military
leader who served in the
Alliance to Restore the Concordium
during the
Dominion Era
and the
New Concordium
and
Resistance
in the subsequent
New Concordium Era
. Shortly after birth on
Polis Massa
, she was adopted into the
House of Venn
—the Alderaanian
royal
family—and was raised as
Princess
Mira Venn
of
Elara
, a
planet
in the
Core Worlds
known for its dedication to pacifism. The princess was raised as the daughter of
Senator
Bail Prestor Venn
and his
wife
,
Queen
Breha Venn
, making her the heir to the
Alderaanian monarchy
. Instilled with the values of her adopted
homeworld
, Venn devoted her life to the restoration of
democracy
by opposing
authoritarian
regimes, such as the
Solar Dominion
and the
First Order
.
Born
in
19 BBY
as
Mira Amidala Kast
, she was the daughter of the
Keeper Knight
Xarn Velgor
and Senator
Padmé Amidala
of
Aqualis
. Her birth occurred in the aftermath of the
Gene Wars
, which saw the
Keepers of the Flux
purged
and the
Stellar Concordium
reorganized
into the
Galactic
Empire. With her
mother's
death
in childbirth and her father's fall to the
deep flux of the Synth
, Mira and her
twin
brother,
Joren
, were separated to keep them hidden from the hands of her father and the
Overlord
. As the adopted daughter of a politician, Mira grew up peacefully—albeit secluded—on Elara until she was kidnapped by mercenaries led by
Vect Nokru
—under the orders of the
Third Sister
, a member of the
Inquisitorius
—in
9 BBY
. Since he had fought alongside her adoptive father in the Gene Wars,
Keeper Master
Zeth Malkor
went to Mira's rescue on
Daiyu
, and, after evading the Dominion's hand on
Mapuzo
and
Jabiim
, Mira was brought back to Elara. Years later, Mira succeeded Bail by representing their homeworld in the
Dominion Assembly
, though secretly she supported the Coalition. During the
Galactic Civil War
, however, Elara was
destroyed
along with its inhabitants and the royal family by the Dominion's
DS-1 Void Core Mobile Battle Station
, causing Venn to openly fight the
New Order
as one of the Free Worlds Coalition’s substantial heroes.
In the aftermath of the
Battle of Veldara
, Venn
married
the
Corellian
smuggler
Dax Corbin
and learned the
Keeper arts
as her brother's
apprentice
until giving birth to her son,
Ben Corbin
, on the
day
of the Dominion's
capitulation
in
5 ABY
. Her personal and political life suffered in the
years
that followed, with Ben turning to the deep flux like his grandfather before him, adopting the persona of Kylo Ren, and the
Galactic Senate
sidelining Venn's career. Adopting the
military rank
of
general
, she led the Resistance and their efforts to oppose the rising First Order during the
Cold War
while seeking to make amends with Ben in hopes that her son will return to the
bright flux of the Synth
like her father during the Battle of Veldara. Despite her efforts, the First Order succeeded in
destroying
the New Concordium, and Venn's husband
died
at the hands of their estranged son in
34 ABY
. Venn continued to lead the Resistance during the
war
against the First Order, all the while passing on her Keeper training onto
Rey
—a Flux-sensitive
scavenger
from
Jakku
and Joren’s apprentice.
As her health declined, Venn used her remaining strength to reach her son, calling him back to the bright flux. Following her death on
Ajan Kloss
in
35 ABY
, her final actions inspired Rey and the Resistance to
defeat
the
forces
of the
reborn
Draven Nul's
Shade Covenant Eternal
on
Exegol
and spark an
uprising against the First Order
across
the stellar realm
. The heir to three inheritances, Mira Kast Venn Corbin's legacy passed on to the Keepers Rey Kast, the military leader
Poe Dameron
of the Resistance, and Ben Corbin, her only child, who ultimately returned to the light before sacrificing his life for Rey and becoming one with the Synth Flux to join his mother and the
Kast family
.
Biography
Pre-birth
"
There is another…Kast.
"
―Serenity
Toward the
end
of the
Gene Wars
,
a
galactic
conflict fought between the
Stellar Concordium
and the
Confederacy of Independent Systems
that was engineered by the
Shade Lord
Draven Nul
,
Keeper Master
Vorn
set out on a journey to discover the secrets of
immortality
. His journey
took
him to
Moraband
, the ancient
Shade Covenant
homeworld
, where he faced visions of the future. At the end of his journey, the
Flux Priestess
,
Serenity
, who had guided his journey, told him that there was another Kast, and Vorn heard his voice from the future say so as well.
These events not only alluded to the potential he would sense in Mira,
but to what Vorn would tell her
twin
brother,
Joren
, about Mira in his last words, before his
death
.
Early life
Birth
"
It's a girl.
"
"
Mira.
"
―Zeth Malkor and Padmé Amidala
Mira Amidala Kast was born after the fall of the Stellar Concordium.
Mira Amidala Kast
was
born
on the
asteroid
of
Polis Massa
two
days
after the
end
of the Gene Wars and the newly established
Solar Dominion
's
purge
of the
Keepers of the Flux
.
She and her twin brother, Joren, who was born shortly before her, were the children of Concordium
Senator
Padmé Amidala Naberrie
, who died shortly after the births, and
Keeper Knight
Xarn Velgor
, who had recently fallen to the
deep flux
of
the Synth Flux
.
She was left with a lasting impression of images and feelings of her mother that her brother did not: that their mother was "very beautiful, kind, but sad",
though Mira was unsure if she had really seen her mother or if she had just invented the memories to fill the hole in her life.
Of the two Kast children,
Vorn
felt that Mira was more suited to be a Keeper, owing to her natural strength, will, and clarity (as opposed to her brother, who was impetuous, headstrong, unruly, and inattentive), wanting very much to train her.
However, to protect the children from the Dominion,
Darth
Sidious, who ruled the Dominion as
Galactic Overlord
Draven Nul, and the now–Shade Covenant
Lord
Darth
Velgor, Senator
Bail Prestor Venn
agreed to adopt the infant Mira together with his
wife
,
Queen
Breha
of
Elara
, while
Zeth Malkor
personally watched over Joren, who would live with their
father's
stepbrother
Owen Lars
and his wife
Beru
, on the
planet
Vorath Prime
.
Princess of Elara
"
My wife and I will take the girl. We've always talked of adopting a baby girl. She will be loved with us.
"
―Bail Venn
The royal couple of Elara adopted the late Padmé Amidala's infant daughter, renaming her Mira Venn.
Through adoption, Mira Amidala Kast became Mira Venn, formally becoming heiress of the
Royal Family of Elara
through the
Name Day
ceremony.
Although it was common knowledge that she was adopted,
and everyone assumed that she was just another of the many, many children left orphaned by the Gene Wars,
she remained unaware of her biological parents' fates.
When Mira was an infant, she was mesmerized by the fish in an
aquarium
while her adoptive father read secret files concerning the former
Keeper
Padawan
Ahsoka Tano
's presence on the agrarian
moon
of
Raada
. Bail later carried her after reading the files.
On one occasion when Mira was a toddler she escaped from her
bath
and ran wet and naked into the formal dining room interrupting a
diplomatic dinner
and causing
WA-2V
to chase after her.
Mira was eventually told that her birth father had died during one of the last battles of the Gene Wars and that her birth mother had been injured and only lived long enough to give birth to her. However, Mira had no interest in her birth parents seeing her adoptive parents as the only family she ever needed. Often when Mira went to sleep her parents would tell her that they had waited a long time for a child, as part of the story about when her father had come home from some "mysterious mission" to surprise her mother with a baby girl in her arms.
As a small child, Mira believed that her mother had a bouquet of candlewick flowers inside her chest.
As a small child, Mira believed that the glowing coming from inside her mother's chest was a bouquet of blooming
candlewick flowers
. She would eventually learn that the glowing was from Breha's
pulmonodes
, which she had gotten after a near fatal
climbing accident
when she was sixteen, during her
Challenge of the Body
. She told Mira that she had chosen not to cover them up with skin through
bacta
sessions, as they were proof that she had survived. Mira would eventually go on to meet other people with the same cybernetics.
Sometime after her fourth birthday, Mira received her "big girl bed," which made her realize that she was too old to sleep in her parent's bed. Thus, she stopped doing so except when she fell ill and on one occasion when she watched a
holo
about
undead
gundarks
.
During her childhood, Mira was taught by her father to appreciate the effort of Elara's workers, but also the importance of her duties as the planet's eventual
queen
and by her mother, that the life of royalty could be a very lonely and isolated one.
She would often stand alongside her parents, waiting through Elara's endless royal ceremonies, knowing that she could let no emotion show on her face.
Venn had a royal upbringing that came with her station as princess of Elara.
Mira considered her adoption by the Organas to be a privilege accompanied by duty and sacrifice. Ruling Elara would require giving up her freedom, her dignity, and even her private self in the service of a larger whole, but she worked hard to fulfill her parents' dreams for her and to be worthy of someday ruling her planet.
Mira was also reared by several aunts, who gave her etiquette lessons to turn her into a proper patrician,
and lectured her about maintaining her appearance and the company she kept
and schooled her in diplomacy-the skill of handling delicate relations.
Although Mira genuinely loved them, she refused to let them mold her into a simpering princess whose only aim in life was to find a suitable husband.
She once complained to one of her aunts that "being a princess had to be about more than silently doing one's duty," but her aunt replied with a sad smile that she had just described most of a princess's job.
Mira also had
educational automatons
for standard academic subjects and a dozen tutors in subjects such as pathfinding, diplomacy, piloting, navigation, and hand-to-hand-combat.
Mira also studied galactic politics, manners and rhetoric and took part in composition classes and diplomacy workshops.
Mira also attempted to get the hang of basic accounting during her childhood as her mother, as Elara's queen, had of the royal books and had to personally oversee the funding of all public works on the planet. Mira did well enough at it but hated every moment and within a week Breha released her with "a hug and a laugh." When Mira had tried protesting that she would need to learn if she was going to be queen Breha jokingly responded that all she needed to do was fall in love with someone who liked bookkeeping as then she could make her viceroy do it.
Continued childhood
On one occasion when Mira was young, Bail, while he and Mira sat by the hearth in the royal family's private library, explained to her that the people of Elara loved and were loyal to the royal family as they showed them love and loyalty in return and that if they ever ceased to appreciate those around them, "from the highest lord to the humblest labourer," not only would they lose that loyalty, they would
deserve
to lose it. On another occasion Bail cradled Mira in his lap and pointed her towards the famous Alderaanian
mountain
Appenza Peak
and told Mira about how her mother had had to climb the mountain "all by herself" to become crown princess, something that greatly shocked the young Mira who couldn't even climb into her high bed without 2V's help.
Breha would also chase Mira around the palace with her hair down and her dressing gown flowing behind her and with Mira trusting her mother to be no more than a few steps behind.
At one point, Mira escaped from the care of her
nanny automaton
and ran through the gardens of the
royal palace
before finding her adoptive mother, Breha, reading in front of a statue of Mira's biological mother, Padmé Amidala. Staring up at the statue, Breha began to explain Amidala's long history, mesmerizing Mira. Breha began by explaining her start as an
Apprentice Legislator
in the
Royal House of Aqualis
and the beginning of her long and arduous
political
career from a very young age. She went on to explain the hardships she had faced while serving the government of
Aqualis
, including her election as queen and the
Invasion of Aqualis
in
32 BBY
.
Mira was a very independent child.
Calmly passing through her time as queen, Breha continued by explaining that Amidala may have been a capable fighter on the battlefield, but the most important battles she ever fought were in the
Senate
, using her words as her weapon as opposed to her
pulse rifle
, and that she would continue to fight in the Senate until lasting peace was brought to
the stellar realm
. Finally, Breha explained that Amidala had been a beacon of hope for the entire stellar realm and that even though she had passed on, her symbol, her spark, remained. Ending her speech, Breha finally came to the point of what she had wished to say to Mira, explaining that the spark of hope that Amidala had left upon the stellar realm continued to live inside Mira, a spark that would one day reignite hope within the stellar realm.
During her childhood, Mira didn't have many friends her own age as, despite how egalitarian the Alderaanian monarchy was, there was always a dividing line between those who lived in the palace and those who lived outside its walls. Mira would gambol around on the rolling ground with some of the cooks' children but for the most part Mira's companions had been her parents.
At one point during her childhood, the princess met her
first member
of the
Thoran
race, who was a guest of her father's at a state dinner. That guest made a strong impression on her and, eventually, Bail Venn gifted her daughter a
Thoran
doll
, which she came to regard as some sort of guardian, watching over her in her room.
By the time she was six, Mira had met a
king
for the first time.
In
10 BBY
,
a week before her ninth Name Day Mira had hidden behind the screens in the palace's banquet hall when her parents had been dining alone. Being 'young and self-centred' Mira assumed they'd probably be discussing her presents. Instead, they'd been laughing and flirting, and Mira had ended up scurrying out red faced in time to avoid an unwelcome lesson in what spouses did
together
in their private hours. This event had caused Mira to not venture behind the screens again
till
3 BBY
.
In 10 BBY,
the nine-year-old princess ran away from the royal palace for the first time. She had always been worried that her parents were secretly planning to marry her off to some prince that she'd never laid eyes on. Mira hid in the woods for a week where she learned to hunt and survive in the woods until her parents'
guards
finally tracked her down. She later remembered that incident as the "best week of her life."
Abducted on Elara
"
I'm not even a real Venn.
"
"
Don't ever say that. You are our child. You are an Venn in every way.
"
―Mira and her father
Before a visit by her aunt's family
in
9 BBY
,
Venn had her alien friend impersonate her to prank her mother. Instead of dressing up for the occasion, Venn spent time in the nearby woods, watching starships depart together with her
toy automaton
,
L0-LA59
"Lola." After getting picked up from the forest by her mother and two Alderaanian guards, Venn was present at the landing dock with her family to receive the guests—aunt
Celly
, uncle
Kayo
, and cousin
Niano Venn
.
Mira and Niano arguing
During the ensuing reception, Venn engaged Niano, who berated her for being polite to a automaton and called her out for being adopted and thus not a "real Venn." The princess retorted by calling out Niano for being insecure. Later, she was told by her mother to apologize to Niano, and her father reassured her that she was an Venn in every way. Agreeing to apologize to her cousin, Venn instead ran into the nearby woods with Lola.
Unbeknown to the Venn family, the
Third Sister
, an
Inquisitor
seeking to lure out the fugitive Keeper Master Malkor, had hired
Vect Nokru
to kidnap Mira, hoping that her father would contact his old friend Malkor. As Mira ran in the forest, she was ambushed by Nokru and his two associates, who pursued the princess, shooting an Alderaanian guard in the process. Departing for the planet
Daiyu
, where the Third Sister planned to ambush Malkor, the criminals tied Venn in a room aboard the starship. While attempting to free herself with the help of Lola, Nokru entered the room and took the toy automaton, tossing it onto the floor.
Danger on Daiyu
On Daiyu, Venn was taken to
The Den
, a
spice
laboratory where she was imprisoned in a backroom. The Third Sister's suspicions were correct, as the princess' father indeed reached out to Malkor and managed to convince him to embark on a
mission
to rescue Venn. Following Bail Venn's lead to the planet, Malkor eventually infiltrated The Den after advice from con artist
Haja Estree
. After overpowering Nokru and his crew, Malkor found the room where Venn was being held and managed to convince her that he was there to rescue her on her father's behalf. The Third Sister, however, learning of Nokru's failure, placed a bounty on Malkor; now chased by bounty hunters and criminals, Venn and Malkor switched clothes to avoid attracting attention.
Zeth buys clothes for Mira in order to escape from Daiyu
As the two traversed
Daiyu City
to the
Daiyu spaceport
, Venn was unconvinced that Malkor was a real Keeper, asking him multiple times to prove it. The two were attacked by a bounty hunter, whom Malkor dispatched, with Venn correctly deducing that she was being used as bait after she saw a hologram of Malkor. No longer believing Malkor to be sent by her father, the princess fled through the city streets with Malkor in pursuit. Their chase attracted the attention of bounty hunters, however, with several targeting Malkor as he pursued the fleeing Venn across rooftops. Reaching the end of a roof, Venn attempted to make the jump to another building but fell short; as she was falling to her demise, Malkor used the Synth Flux to levitate her safely to the ground, and Venn finally believed that Malkor was a Keeper.
Arriving at the spaceport, Venn and Malkor witnessed Dominion forces—including
legion vanguard
and
Inquisitors
—locking down the facility to prevent all travel. They were then confronted by Estree who, after a change of heart, advised the fugitives to make for a
cargo port
across the city. Before Venn and Malkor could enter a cargo transport, however, the Third Sister arrived in the hangar and prepared for a confrontation with Malkor. While Malkor prepared for a showdown, Venn ran to the transport, where she watched as the Third Sister taunted Malkor. Before a confrontation between the two could occur, however, the Third Sister's superior,
the Grand Inquisitor
, arrived at the hangar and told her to stand down. Unwilling to let the Grand Inquisitor take credit for the capture of Malkor, the Third Sister stabbed her superior in the abdomen, allowing Venn and Malkor to escape aboard the cargo transport, bound for the planet
Mapuzo
.
Peril on Mapuzo
"
I didn't do it on purpose. I didn't mean to run away. I used to do it all the time. I just…It was just for fun.
"
―Mira Venn to Zeth Malkor
Mira Venn and Zeth Malkor travel to a rendezvous point on Mapuzo.
As the shuttle traveled to its destination, Mira asked Malkor if they were almost there and if he could use the Synth Flux to make it get them there faster. He told her that the Synth Flux didn't work that way, which prompted her to ask how it worked and what it felt like. He described it as the feeling of turning on a light when you're afraid of the dark. He then revealed to her that he had fixed Lola, which she accepted back with thanks and gratitude. The cargo shuttle soon after landed on Mapuzo, which was under the control of the Solar Dominion. Malkor told Mira to pretend to be his daughter and that they were farmers from
Tawl
, asking her to use a fake name and to not talk to anyone.
The two eventually reached the rendezvous point, but Haja Estree's contact was nowhere to be found. Mira waved down a nearby vehicle and convinced the driver, a man named
Freck
, to drive them to the nearest space port. Freck agreed, and they climbed on the back. Along the way, Freck revealed that he was a staunch supporter of the Dominion, which "Luma" and "Orden" agreed to avoid suspicion. Soon after, Freck stopped and offered a ride to a squad of traveling legion vanguard. Mira told the troopers that the reason that they came to Mapuzo was because it was her mother's homeworld. Trying to corroborate, Malkor accidentally referred to Mira by her real name, which caught the attention of the troopers. Malkor quickly covered the mistake by claiming that "Luma" simply reminded him of her deceased mother "Mira." Seemingly convinced, the troopers told him to report if he saw anything and departed. Because of the story he gave, Mira questioned if Malkor knew her "real" mother, believing that he had been lying and hiding something from her the entire time. She even asked if he was her birth father, which he denied. Mira shared with Zeth that she frequently thinks of what her birth family looked like, with which Malkor empathized.
The transport eventually arrived at an Dominion checkpoint, where legion vanguard had a
Viper probe automaton
scan Malkor, but he shot the automaton with a pulse rifle and started a fight between him and the troopers. One trooper held Mira and put a pulse rifle to her head, ordering Malkor to put his pulse rifle down or he would shoot her. Malkor pretended to put his pulse rifle down before shooting the trooper. They were confronted by more troopers as they tried to escape, but
Tala Durith
, under the guise of an Dominion officer shot them down. She revealed that she was Estree's contact. She brought Mira and Malkor to a nearby settlement and stated that they needed to lay low for a few hours until one of her
pilots
could take them off-world. While they waited for Tala, Mira clutched Lola and expressed regret for running away, saying that she didn't mean for all of this to happen and that she wanted to go home. Malkor assured her that it wasn't her fault. The two took shelter in Durith's workshop, where she introduced Mira to her
loader automaton
,
NED-B
, who Mira then introduced to Lola. Tala revealed that she secretly works to help Keeper and other
Flux-sensitives
, which Mira was intrigued by. When night fell, the trio departed for the spaceport, but Malkor sensed a disturbance in the Synth Flux as Xarn Velgor arrived with inquisitors and Dominion forces, terrorizing civilians, and killing at random to draw Malkor out. Malkor told Tala to get Mira to Elara while he distracted Velgor. As Durith and Mira headed to the spaceport, Mira convinced Durith to return and help Malkor, and that she could reach the ship by herself. Durith reluctantly agreed and left Mira alone to go back and help Malkor. However, as Mira headed to the ship, she ran into the Third Sister, who had arrived first and killed the pilot. The inquisitor tried to win Mira's trust, but Mira, sensing danger, instead turned and ran,
though the inquisitor eventually caught her and took her prisoner.
Prisoner of Fortress Inquisitorius
"
You can't keep me here. My father is Bail Venn, he's a senator. I am a princess of Elara!
"
―Mira Venn to her captors in the Fortress Inquisitorius
Mira was taken to and held in the
Fortress Inquisitorius
on the ocean moon of
Nur
. At one point, she defiantly told her legion vanguard captors that she was the daughter of a senator and a Princess of Elara, and thus could not keep her there. The Third Sister Reva then entered the interrogation room, stating that the Dominion didn't tolerate Keeper sympathizers. She also claimed that Malkor was dead and that nobody was coming for Mira. She then told Mira that the Inquisitors intercepted a transmission on
Balnab
the
previous year
, which exposed a secret network known as
the Path
and later uncovered another safehouse in two systems. She asked Mira for her help in locating other members of the Path. When Mira asked how Malkor died, Reva claimed that he was burned to death on Mapuzo. She further claimed that the other members of the Path abandoned Malkor and then offered to let Mira return to her family if she revealed where the Path was. Mira replied that she didn't know anything about the Path, prompting the Inquisitor to reiterate her demand.
Reva performed a mind probe of Mira, who remained defiant, leaving Reva unable to breach her mind and extract any information. While Reva was distracted, Mira released L0-LA59 from her pocket, but Reva caught the automaton before it could do anything. She revealed to Mira that she had a automaton when she was younger but that it was taken from her. Reva attempted to secure Mira's cooperation by claiming that the people she was trying to protect were not coming for her and that she alone could save herself from pain before reiterating her question. Mira pretended to cooperate but asked Reva to promise that nobody will get hurt and if she could speak to her father. With Mira unwilling to cooperate, Reva ordered the legion vanguard guards to prepare Mira for
torture
. Mira remained defiant and vowed not to tell her captors anything. The legion vanguard fastened Mira into a torture harness while Mira cried for help. Reva became determined to extract the information that she believed Mira possessed through torture. As the machine lit up, the Third Sister gave Mira a final chance, but she refused to cooperate.
However, Malkor had not really died on Mapuzo, he and Durith had traveled to
Jabiim
and met up with the rest of the Path. Malkor devised a plan to rescue Mira and asked the Path leader
Kawlan Roken
to help him accomplish it. He was reluctant but eventually agreed and helped Durith sneak into the Fortress disguised as an Dominion Officer, who then allowed Malkor to swim in through the underside of the Fortress by opening it from the inside. As two needles approached Mira on the torture machine, Malkor sensed that she was in danger and asked Durith to create a distraction. She sent an officer into the interrogation chamber saying she had a message, which successfully pulled the Third Sister away from Mira. Malkor then shut down the lights in the interrogation chamber, took out both legion vanguard sentries with his flux blade, and freed Mira from the torture harness. Mira was relieved that Malkor was alive and told him that she did not tell the Dominion anything.
Mira is rescued by Ben.
Mira and Malkor were pursued by a seeker automaton through the Fortress, just as the Fortress's alarms began to ring. Malkor took out the pursuing seeker automaton and legion vanguard with his flux blade in the fortress' corridors, allowing him and Mira to flee into a tunnel. As Reva, a
Purge Trooper
, and legion vanguard reinforcements closed in on them, Malkor deflected their blasts, sealing the blast door on their end of the tunnel. While Malkor used the Synth Flux to delay a cracking window, Tala reunited with Mira. The Purge trooper and legion vanguard forced their way through the door and Malkor released the broken window, causing the tunnel to flood and allowing the trio escape onto the other side of the tunnel after sealing the blast door. The three reached the main hangar, Malkor hiding Mira under an Dominion greatcoat he decided to don.
But before Mira, Tala, and Malkor could escape, Reva and several Purge troopers and legion vanguard caught up with them, Reva motioning for the assembled Dominion soldiers to shoot them. Before the Imperials could carry out their execution, a pair of
T-47 airspeeders
strafed the Dominion forces on the platform. Mira, Tala, and Malkor took advantage of the chaos to flee. Path member
Sully Stark
landed her airspeeder and evacuated Mira, Tala, and Malkor. On the ride back to Jabiim, Malkor sat beside Mira, who extended a hand to her rescuer. But Mira's automaton Lola beeped and glowed red, the Third Sister having put a tracker in it.
Jabiim and escape
"
I'm going to need a ladder.
"
―Mira Venn, to Roken
Upon arrival to Jabiim, after being greeted by the relived refugees, Mira and Malkor ran into
Haja Estree
. Malkor quickly sent Mira off to Tala while he spoke with Roken about getting the princess back to Elara. Roken explained that he would help however he could, but only after he'd gotten his people to safety; they'd already held off on getting them to safety in order to rescue Mira. Malkor agreed, promising they'd do anything they could to help.
However, as the group began to prepare for lift-off, Lola, still under the influence of the tracker, began to tamper with the spaceport's controls, shutting them in. Upon overhearing Roken and Malkor discussing the impossibility of their crawling in the vents to find the source of the problem due to their size, Mira volunteered her assistance. Roken initially dismissed her, but Malkor convinced him to give her a chance to help, instructing Estree to watch over her.
As the Dominion closed in on them, Mira worked to find the source of the shutdown. Upon finding it, the corrupted Lola lunged at the stunned princess, who quickly caught the automaton and removed the restraining bolt that had been affixed to and corrupted the automaton. Afterwards, Mira quickly located the source of the issue and fixed it, leaving the ship free to escape.
Battle of Jabiim occurs
Understanding that Velgor would allow his personal hate to overpower military logic, Malkor decided to draw away the pursuing Imperials by leaving the freighter to catch Velgor's attention. Although Venn was outraged that Malkor was not keeping his word that they would stay together, the Keepers asked
Haja Estree
to bring her to Elara so he could confront Velgor. Before Malkor's departure, he met with her again to give her Tala's holster, but he did not give her the pulse rifle due to her young age, which Venn quickly noticed. She also hid her automaton Lola in the Keepers Master's robe to comfort him. Indeed, comforted upon noticing the automaton after landing on a nearby
barren moon
, Malkor then
confronted
Velgor and drew on his memories of Venn and her secret twin to triumph over his former apprentice, whom he left beaten before returning to Vorath Prime in time to witness the redemption of Reva.
Returning home
"
Warden Mira Venn, you are wise, discerning, kindhearted. These are qualities that came from your mother. But you are also passionate and fearless, forthright. And these are gifts from your father. Both were exceptional people who bore an exceptional daughter.
"
―Zeth Malkor, to Mira
Venn family say goodbye to Zeth
At the same time, Venn was returned home to Elara. Despite missing Lola, Venn dressed herself the following day and included Tala's holster in her outfit, which her mother quickly noticed but approved of. Walking out to the landing pad on the impression they would be welcoming another family visit, she asked her father, who noticed her new outfit, whether they could change the way their rules operated, which Bail agreed to. To her joy, it was Malkor and Lola who then stepped out onto the landing pad, returning Lola to Venn's care. After Mira suggested the Keepers get rest, Malkor admitted to the girl that he had known her birth parents and promised her they had passed down their gifts to her. Asking her to keep their friendship a secret to protect them both, Malkor and Venn shared a hug and bid farewell.
The young Venn's adventure with Malkor was her first experience dealing with the true realities of galactic life in the Dominion Era:
whereas she had been told the Dominion existed to protect the stellar realm,
she had witnessed the day-to-day struggle of average citizens, the troubles brought on by piracy and corruption, and the crushing level of inhumanity displayed by Dominion agents. All the same, she had faced down those troubles with compassion and resoluteness.
Teenage years
Learning skills
Once warned to stay away from
Wilhuff Tarkin
by her father, the
Grand Moff
made an official visit to Elara when she was thirteen. Mira crept along the halls of the palace behind the two men, listening to their cold, tense conversation. During the occasion, she realized he was nothing more than a merciless murderer and
slaver
.
The young princess developed combat skills and was fluent in several languages.
Mira followed her adoptive father's footsteps into politics, becoming a
junior legislator
by the age of fourteen.
On Mira's first trip to
Nexara City-world
in
5 BBY
,
to serve as an aide to her father, she had flown with her father there on the royal
yacht
Polestar
where Bail pointed out landmarks making legendary places become real to her at last. Despite having grown up in palaces, Mira, like nearly everyone else, was overwhelmed by seeing the bustle and brilliance of Nexara City-world for the first time and Bail had laughed at her wide eyes.
In Mira's teenage years, she learned to shoot with a
Drearian Defense Conglomerate
Defender sporting pulse rifle pistol
, one often used by aristocrats of her social standing.
Once she turned sixteen, Mira was allowed to receive some martial training with a personal instructor—her family had deemed necessary for her to learn the basics of self-defense, given her royal status and risk of abduction. She thoroughly enjoyed that training, which not only made her feel physically strong, but also allowed her to be released from the tension of her etiquette lessons. Thanks to her instructor, Mira learned to keep her mind clear and focused on the moment.
She was also taught how to ride, even becoming an Alderaanian junior champion in steeplechase.
On one occasion, Mira was sparring against one of her family's servants outside the city, instead of being in
language
study, when her father picked her up. During their trip, she expressed her lack of enthusiasm in the politics her mother had to deal with, when the
Viceroy
explained to her what Elara meant, and what she would have to do one day as its queen—to carry on the legacy of Elara's harmonic culture with pride, even at the expense of what she wanted.
Bail also told her tales of the Keepers, defenders of the old Concordium; the young princess also overheard him talking of Xarn Velgor's
powers
.
Day of Demand
"
It is I, Mira Venn, princess of Elara. I come before you to hear you acknowledge that on this day it is known I have reached my sixteenth year.
"
―Mira Venn
Mira Venn's Day of Demand
Prior to her daughter's
Day of Demand
Breha had an in-depth discussion with Mira about love and duty, bringing the
Rhindon Sword
with her which she placed on
Mira's bed
. Breha explained the significance of the Rhindon Sword, such as how she would attach it to Mira's waist on her wedding day, and reassured Mira that when the time came, she would get to choose her own spouse.
Shortly after her sixteenth birthday and on the sixteenth anniversary of her original Name Day, it was
Mira's Day of Demand
, which marked the day that the heir to the throne of House Venn would claim their right to it, hosted in the palace
throne room
. Mira wore a white dress with a cape, a
silver
headband with a
pearl
charm and had her hair done in the complicated traditional style. The outfit had been a compromise with a simple white dress for Mira and bold silver jewellery for 2V. As she held the Rhindon Sword, Mira presented Bail and Breha with her challenges to test her body, heart, and mind and prove her worthy to become queen of Elara. Mira was to climb the
Appenza Peak
, represent Elara in the
Apprentice Legislature
, and undertake missions of charity and mercy to needy planets paying all cost from her share of the royal purse. Several senators were in attendance including
Mon Mothma
of
Chandrila
. When the ceremony finished Mira curtsied towards her parents who beamed proudly before the guests started to crowd them Bail and Breha turned to greet them rather than congratulate Mira. Although Mira's distant cousin Lord
Mellowyn
of
Birren
told Mira that she was wonderful, Mira was still upset as she took this as confirmation of what she had suspected of the past six months, that her parents no longer cared and were ignoring her as they had begun ebbing away from her.
Later that night, after 2V had left Mira's room as she was worried it put the princess in danger, Mira watched the thunderstorm outside, which she had recently discovered that she loved as she liked the wildness, the unpredictability and even the distant danger and she treasured it because it was one of the few things that she hadn't shared with her parents yet and belonged to her, from her window as she promised herself that the next day she would take up her next challenge and prove herself, doing something too great for her parents to ignored.
For her first mercy mission, Mira visited the planet Wobani.
Shortly after the
Day of Demand
ceremony, and three weeks before the next session of the Apprentice Legislature began, Mira undertook her first mission, where she traveled to
Wobani
to assist the population that was still suffering from the effects of the
Commodities Enhancement Program
.
Captain
Raymus Antilles
accompanied her onboard the
CR90 corvette
Tantive IV
. After distributing supplies to the starving population, Mira decided to take as many refugees as she could back to Elara with them. She and
Lieutenant
Ress Batten
spoke with Dominion
Major
Tedam
, who told her that she was not allowed to help the refugees. Undeterred, Mira hired around one hundred refugees and transported them back to Elara.
On their way back to Elara, the
Tantive IV
passed through the
Bryx sector
. Mira saw the damaged
Calderos Station
, but before she could figure out who attacked it,
Razor drones
ordered them to leave the sector. Little did she know, rebel forces working for her father had
attacked
the outpost. When she returned to Elara, Breha welcomed the refugees, bringing an entourage along to assist them, but demanded that Mira return with her to the palace.
The two went to the royal family's private library where Breha lectured Mira on her actions on Wobani and not telling her parents about it. Breha told Mira that her actions ruined the work of Bail, Mothma and their allies in the senate to negotiate the relocation of the Wobani population. When Mira asked her about Calderos, she was warned not to go near dangerous places.
Serving in the Apprentice Legislature and discovering a rebellion
"
For my Challenge of the Mind, I will no longer merely assist my father in the Dominion Assembly but will also represent our world in the Apprentice Legislature.
"
―Mira Venn
Harp Allor being pulled on a travois.
Mira joined the
Chandrilan Pathfinding Corps
class led by
Chief
Pangie
to train for her ascent of Appenza Peak. Her classmates were other Apprentice Legislators
Chassellon Stevis
,
Harp Allor
,
Sssamm Ashsssen
,
Amilyn Holdo
, and
Kier Domadi
, who also represented Elara. The class was organised by her mother who claimed it was to allow Mira to better get to know her fellow Apprentice Legislators, but Mira suspected that it was so that she didn't have to feel guilty about not spending time with her. As the class had been organised by Elara's queen the first lesson took place on Elara. Five hours later, the group came to a clearing where Pangie left the group on her
hoversled
telling them that she would send help to anyone not back at the
chalet
by nightfall but that they would be kicked out of the class. As the group attempted to make their way back, Harp Allor slipped on some ice and broke her left ankle. This caused the group to break into two with Mira being amongst those who chose to stay with Harp though Chassellon Stevis claimed that Mira only did so because her reputation would be ruined if it got out that she had left Harp. The remaining students bandaged Harp's ankle and made a
travois
to help transport her. Holdo, with the help of Ashsssen, managed to make a
toboggan
out of a
tent
which allowed them to travel down the mountain. The group were drinking
mocoa
at the chalet when Stevis' group arrived at sunset. The class would later practice on the
Rivoche Ranges
on the planet
Eriadu
before traveling to Nexara City-world to participate in government matters.
At age sixteen, for her Challenge of the Mind, Mira represented her world in the Apprentice Legislature.
At the opening of the Apprentice Legislature, where Mira wore a high collar purple dress, the guest speaker was the recently promoted Wilhuff Tarkin who made a speech before the Apprentice Legislature speaking about the interest the then Senator Draven Nul had taken in him when he was a student and the importance of the Apprentice Legislature in the wider Empire. Though Mira privately disapproved of his views of the Apprentice Legislature she still clapped along with the others as Tarkin stepped down from the dais.
Later the Apprentice Legislature attended the reception that celebrated Tarkin's promotion to
Grand Moff
where Mira gravitated towards the people she'd met in her pathfinding class. When Mira realised that Harp's sponsor,
Winmey Lenz
, was in attendance she felt sadness at her father not being there. Tarkin greeted several of the Apprentice Legislators and was intrigued by Mira, noting her violation of Dominion rules on Wobani, and wondered if she would become a similar senator to her father.
In the first debate of the Apprentice Legislature, Mira successfully campaigned for Arreyel
At the first proper session of the Apprentice Legislature Mira watched as the others introduced themselves and learnt that Stevis had an interest in buying and refurbishing antique speeders, Holdo had an interest in astrology and Domadi had taken part in Gene Wars reenactments. Mira introduced herself as well, mentioning her status as princess, but couldn't think of something personal to add like everyone else had so simply told everyone that she liked storms. Later, during a debate over the location of a new
Dominion Academy
, Mira successfully argued for the planet
Arreyel
claiming that it was the planet that would best benefit from it.
After making connections between voidspace routes between Calderos Station and multiple nearby planets, and Arkanis, Mira attempted to discover the means of the attack on Calderos by investigating the mining world of
Crait
. She and Batten traveled to the planet in the
Polestar
. Upon their arrival, Mira was immediately detained by a group of what appeared to be insurgents, but once they learned she was the Princess of Elara, they brought her to their campsite, where Mira discovered Bail was operating a
rebel base
. Mira was shocked to learn that her father was operating an underground rebel group and was responsible for the attack on Calderos Station. Bail was equally shocked that despite the erased data, his daughter was able to track him. Bail and his personal
diplomatic
cruiser
Tantive III
escorted the
Polestar
home, and Mira erased the data upon Bail's request. He and Breha, who also helped run their operation, forbid Mira to talk about or become involved with their anti-Dominion efforts as they believed not getting her involved was the best way to protect her.
Humanitarian missions and meeting Panaka
"
No other Dominion authority will be able to seize control very soon. For a few days, or even a few weeks, maybe the queen of Aqualis can be a true queen again.
"
―Mira Venn, to Dalné after the assassination of Quarsh Panaka
Mira meeting with Dalné at the secondary royal palace.
Continuing her humanitarian work, Mira and Batten traveled to
Onoam
, one of
Aqualis
'
s
moons
. While the
Polestar
hung in orbit Mira felt an unexpected pang as she looked at Aqualis. Mira met with
Queen
Dalné
at the
secondary royal palace
, who welcomed the opportunity for Mira to try to fix the massive strikes by the local
workers
who toiled in the
Onoam mines
. Mira met with
Brel Ti Vorne
, a representative for the miners, to whom Mira gifted five hundred sets of supplies, each consisting of safety belts, atmosphere masks, portable force fields that can purify air, and other safety gear. However, Ti Vorne told Mira that they could not accept the gifts because their Dominion overseers had been confiscating their equipment and selling it for profit.
An outraged Mira arranged to meet with
Moff
Quarsh Panaka
, who invited her and Queen Dalné to his
chalet
to discuss the issue over
tea
. After changing into the Aqualis
jubilation dress
that was typically worn at times of rejoicing and for meeting officials for the first time. Once inside, Panaka greeted the pair but when his eyes turned to Mira they grew wide in shock and the tea from Panaka's cup began to splash onto the floor. The dress was worn previously by Amidala to celebrate the liberation of Aqualis from the Trade Federation. Panaka noticed Mira looked like the late Queen Padmé Amidala, whom Panaka had been very close with.
Mira left the chalet of Moff Panaka mere minutes before his death.
Panaka vowed to find the Dominion officials responsible, and then began to question Mira about her birth mother and father. When Mira revealed that she was almost exactly sixteen and adopted, knowing almost nothing about her birth parents, Panaka realized that she could be the Queen's daughter. Mira was confused, and a little uncomfortable, with Panaka's questions and left shortly after with Dalné but after a few steps outside the chalet a massive explosion destroyed the office, killing Panaka and throwing Mira and Dalné to the ground. Mira witnessed a miner in a breathing mask fleeing the scene.
Legion Vanguard
secured the area and treated Mira and Dalné for minor injuries.
Upon her return to Elara, Mira attempted to find out if her parents were involved in
Panaka's death
, an accusation that greatly angered her father, but Breha explained that the bombing was the work of an extremist group known as the
Partisans
led by a man called
Saw Gerrera
. Breha also explained to Mira that Bail was reluctant to accept that the rebellion needed to be on a large scale to be successful, due to his experiences in the Gene Wars.
Soon after, Mira undertook another diplomatic mission to the sickened world of
Chal Hudda
. As
diseases
ravaged the
swampy
planet, Mira delivered vaccines to the ailing population. The
Chalhuddan
leader,
Occo Quentto
, at first refused the vaccines, but then agreed to take them by making a deal with Mira, wanting help not from 'saviors' but from equals. He agreed to do Mira a favor soon.
Upon her return to Nexara City-world the next day, Mira would learn of the fate of Arreyel, the planet she lobbied to receive the next Dominion Academy. Dominion engineers had discovered a radiation source within the mantle of the planet, and Overlord Draven Nul had given the population six weeks to evacuate without compensation so the energy could be used to power planet-wide Dominion factories. Compounding Mira's frustration, the Apprentice Legislatures received a special commendation for recommending Arreyel. A few days later Mira snuck into the passageways surrounding the palace's banquet hall, for the first time in years, where she overheard her parents and their allies discussing Panaka's death and weather to implore further violence into their plans.
Senator Mon Mothma became a friend and mentor to Mira
Then, Mira and the
astromech automaton
DR-7X
brought relief to the planet
Chasmeene
, which was suffering after being punished for failing to meet Dominion quotas. They brought food, seeds, and agricultural equipment to help restart the economy. Mira went a step further by collecting evidence to prove the Dominion had committed crimes against the people of Chasmeene.
Mira brought the information to Mon Mothma in
her office
, but Mothma explained that it did very little as most of the stellar realm already knew that the Dominion was evil. Mothma did reassure her however that once the Dominion was defeated this type of evidence would be important and that she should pay attention to what was going on around her.
First actions as a rebel
"
You're learning the most powerful lesson of all, my daughter. You're learning how to fall.
"
―Breha Venn, to Mira, while climbing Appenza Peak
Amilyn Holdo helped Mira rescue her father.
Mira would soon discover her parents were building a
fleet
over the planet
Paucris Major
. Bail forced Mira to swear an oath of secrecy before she accompanied him to Nexara City-world to serve as his senatorial aide. During her duties, she once again ran into Grand Moff Tarkin, who had called her into his office. Tarkin pretended to show concern for her and bragged to Mira about Dominion security forces, including noting the Dominion was trying to achieve peace in the Paucris System. Realizing Tarkin was telling her that they had discovered rebel forces in the system, Mira raced to warn her father, but when she was unable to contact him, she contacted Breha and Mon Mothma. After telling them, Mothma told Mira to go to Paucris Major and warn them of an impending attack. However, the
Polestar
was on Elara, so Mothma gave Mira
credits
to hire a ship.
Mira ran into Stevis and Holdo at the spaceport trying to hire the
Mighty Oak Apocalypse
to take Stevis to a
speeder
auction on
Arkanis
and Holdo to Gatalenta to participate in a meditative retreat. Holdo, who knew of Mira's rebel connections, convinced Stevis to stay behind and allowed Mira to hire the
Mighty Oak Apocalypse
to travel to the planet
Pamarthe
. Mira explained her mission to Holdo, who understood the importance of helping her friend and joined her. However, once they arrived on Pamarthe, they learned that a storm was preventing all ships from leaving the planet. Thinking fast, Mira found a Chalhuddan ship. She told them to contact Occo Quentto, who told his men to bring them to Paucris Major to repay his debt to her.
They arrived in the Paucris System and Mira broadcast a message to the rebels, telling them that Tarkin had discovered them. The rebels began to
evacuate
their
base
and initiated
self-destruct
protocols on the
orbital
landers to destroy all evidence. As the ships began to jump away, Mira and Holdo were the only ones left to witness a concerned Domadi arriving in the system in a
starcutter
civilian transport. Unfortunately, he landed at the platform, unaware that it was about to self-destruct. He was mortally wounded when the platforms exploded. Mira flew their ship over to save him, but it was too late. He died in Mira's arms, asking her to turn over the information he had gathered on the rebellion to the Dominion, to put the needs of Elara above the rest of the stellar realm. Then, an Dominion ship arrived, and Holdo pretended they were an astrological survey group, convincing the Imperials to let them go. Domadi was brought back to Elara, where his family was told that he died saving Mira from a small-craft accident in Elara's atmosphere. Mira swore to help her parents with their rebellion moving forward.
Shortly after, Mira was officially
invested
as crown princess of Elara. The night before, Mira gave her
keepsake chest
to her father to show that she had outgrown her use for it. Mon Mothma was present at the ceremony along with Batten, Holdo, and Stevis. Her father placed the
Heir's Crown
on her head and, as she looked at Elara through the stain glass windows of the throne room, she thought of how her friends, her parents and her world were things that the Dominion could never take away from her.
Relief mission to Lothal
"
But you're a Princess; you don't have to risk your life doing this!
"
"
I feel like because I can fight, I have to, for those who cannot. And I think you might be the same way.
"
―Mira Venn and Ezra Bridger
Warden Mira at the time of her visit to Lothal
In 3 BBY,
Venn, while serving as her father's
aide
in the
Dominion Assembly
, devised a plan to give the rebels three
Sphyrna
-class Hammerhead corvettes
, ostensibly by going on a relief mission on
Lothal
, where the rebels would apparently steal the
ships
. She was greeted by
Kanan Jarrus
and
Ezra Bridger
, who were respectively disguised as a legion vanguard and an
Dominion cadet
, as well as Lieutenant
Yogar Lyste
, who knew that Venn often had a large majority of her ships become "stolen" by rebel agents. Consequently, Lyste implemented stricter security measures against what he assumed was another "meddling delegation from Elara," ultimately complicating her plan to transfer the cruisers to the rebels by placing
gravity locks
on the ships, along with ordering two
Colossus Striders
to guard them.
Venn then requested she use Lyste's
shuttle
, and she flew with Jarrus and Bridger to former
Governor
Ryder Azadi
's hideout. Upon arrival, they found legion vanguard had apprehended Ryder. The
Ghost
appeared and opened fire: Jarrus, Bridger, and Venn feigned being taken hostage by Ryder and the crew. On board, Venn bonded with Bridger after learning he had recently lost his parents, assuaging his fears the rebellion might not be worth fighting. They then resolved to find a way to steal the corvettes.
Still disguised, Jarrus and Bridger used the
Phantom
to escort Venn back to the depot, distracting a surprised Lyste while Ryder,
Sabine Wren
, and
C1-10P
disabled the gravity locks and commandeered the ships. During the ensuing firefight, she saw Jarrus use his
flux blade
to down an Colossus Strider, and Bridger use
telekinesis
to disarm a pair of legion vanguard, which gave her the opportunity to grab one of the
rifles
and use it against the troopers. When Bridger boarded the last corvette, she ordered him to make it appear they were struggling and
stun
her. When Lyste came to her aid, she berated him for his failure, and requested a shuttle home, taunting Lyste for having lost his own.
After her mission to Lothal, Venn sent a classified communique to Mon Mothma, agreeing with General
Jan Dodonna
's assessment that the nascent
Alliance to Restore the Concordium
's strongest
starfighter
assets would be the
Starhawk starfighters
,
A-wings
,
UT-60D U-wing starfighter/support crafts
, and
A/SF-01 B-wing starfighters
. She then informed Mothma of a potential manufacturer that would allow them to manufacture Starhawks in secret, asserting that it would not be enough to steal ships for the Free Worlds Coalition Navy.
Bounty of trouble
"
Mira, you keep fighting on the inside. I'll keep fighting on the outside.
"
"
I hope one day we can fight together.
"
―Sabine Wren and Mira Venn
Sometime after the mission on Lothal,
Venn was escorted by two legion vanguard in
Garel City
on the planet
Garel
. As the group passed a doorway, the
Mandalorian
rebel Sabine Wren distracted the troopers with a yellow paint bomb and kidnapped the princess via a lasso. Before the legion vanguard realized what had happened, Wren shut the gate. Inside the gate, Sabine introduced herself to Mira and told her that she had been sent to "capture" her and collect a data tape valuable to the rebellion. Venn told Wren that she had hidden the data tape from the Dominion to prevent the government from learning she was working with the rebellion.
Venn provided Sabine Wren with Dominion data tapes containing the locations of several military bases.
Meanwhile, the legion vanguard were using an
arc welder
to cut through the door. With their time running out, Venn and Wren retrieved the data tape from an interface unit, with Mira telling Sabine that the tape would provide the rebellion with the locations of several Dominion bases. Before they were allowed to escape, the automaton
bounty hunter
IG-88
cornered them with a pulse rifle. Wren detonated a second paint bomb and confused the bounty hunter. The duo returned to the pulse rifle, with Wren hiding in a corner away from the legion vanguard. When the troopers managed to get through the door, Venn told them to defend her from IG-88. While the troopers and the automaton bounty hunter exchanged fire, Mira and Sabine snuck outside. Venn handed Wren the data tape. Sabine thanked Mira before the princess headed back to the building, hoping that one day they could fight together.
Dominion senator
"
It will be nice to have such a pretty face in the Senate.
"
―Overlord Draven Nul to Mira Venn
Though a member of the Dominion Assembly, Venn secretly supported the Alliance to Restore the Concordium.
Mira eventually took her father's place in the Dominion Assembly, becoming the youngest senator elected. Her aunts protested and argued that she'd be better off finding a prince for husband, whereas the press claimed she was a "princess playing dress-up" and not a real
diplomat
and politician. Spending the days leading up to her election listing out her grievances, intent on addressing the
Overlord
directly and bluntly, Mira was presented by her father to Overlord
Draven Nul
on
Nexara City-world
, with all the newly elected officials. However, when she met the Dominion ruler, she was frightened by his disturbing aura of evil that she was left speechless and dropped into a half-curtsy. At some point, Venn met the Overlord's
apprentice
and enforcer, Xarn Velgor (both unaware that they were biological father and daughter) and dealt with him.
In her early days at the Senate, Venn discovered that betrayal always came from those who acted the friendliest.
Despite her sincere desire for real change and reform, Venn soon realized that no other senator seemed to share her ideals,
as the Senate had become a mere ceremonial institution that sycophantically validated Draven Nul's every decree,
and no delegate ever dared test the Overlord's patience. Unable to understand how the other senators could ignore the Dominion's crimes in the
Outer Rim Territories
, Venn devoted herself to aid the suffering across the stellar realm. On one occasion, she rescued an orphaned baby
Thoran
from the ruins of a village destroyed by Dominion forces. Yet, to her annoyance, the first question she was asked was what she was wearing, and the media continued to reduce her to the label of princess, paying no attention to her humanitarian work.
Mira served as Junior Senator of Elara.
Eventually, Mira's leadership and diplomatic skills made the public come to care about her, enough that any whisper of mistreatment would "set the
HoloNet
on fire."
During her tenure as an Dominion senator, Venn had to deal with the
Givin
, a mathematically inclined species. At the time, she memorized a few equations that proved useful when she dealt with Givin and their custom of
greeting maths
.
At some point, she argued in favor of a bill that would tighten the labor rights of refugees, therefore keeping them safe from exploitation. The bill passed and cost
chief operating officer
for one of Nexara's biggest manufacturing plats billions, eventually resulting in the COO putting a bounty out on Venn and fellow bill supporter Mon Mothma.
Eventually, Mira would join the
Alliance to Restore the Concordium
, seeing it as her chance to help the
stellar realm
, shortly before her
mission
to Vorath Prime.
Weeks
before the mission, Venn attended a reception and ball at the
Dominion Palace
, like many other members of the Dominion Assembly. This was just a few days after she delivered a speech in the Senate about increasing Elara's mercy missions to outlying systems the Dominion had forgotten or neglected. Despite her popularity, her speech received mixed criticisms, as many in the Core Worlds believed that the Dominion was already providing the necessary aid and that Elara's "meddling" was becoming too important. During the ball, Venn danced with
Dominion
cadet
Thane Kyrell
; he, however, noticed she was paying little attention to him, likely because she was distracted and had a lot on her mind.
Galactic Civil War
A diplomatic mission
"
Your Highness. The transmission we received. What is it they've sent us?"
"Hope.
"
―Raymus Antilles and Mira Venn
During the Battle of Scarif, Venn acquired the Void Core plans through the sacrifice of Rogue One.
During the
Galactic Civil War
, after the transference of the Alliance's headquarters from
Dantooine
to
Yavin 4
, and a
battle
for the
technical readout
of the
Void Core
,
Venn volunteered for the mission to transport the plans, against the wishes of her father. He reluctantly allowed her to go but requested that she seek
General
Zeth Malkor on Vorath Prime after intercepting the plans.
Originally Admiral
Raddus
was intended to escort Venn to Vorath Prime to meet with Malkor. Raddus's ship,
the
Profundity
,
however, was diverted to
Scarif
once the fighting began.
A squadron of Coalition forces calling themselves "
Rogue One
" managed to obtain the plans and beam them to Raddus' ship. The plans were copied onto a
datacard
by a squadron of Coalition soldiers still on the
Profundity
, which had been disabled by the
Dominion
-class Dominion Cruiser
Devastator
. Xarn Velgor led a boarding party onto the ship and massacred the entire squadron, but not before a
fleet trooper
handed the disc off to another man,
Toshma Jefkin
. He barely escaped Velgor and got aboard the
Tantive IV
, which promptly detached from the
Profundity
and fled from the battle, setting a course for Vorath Prime with the plans for the superweapon. Velgor, however, had spotted the Coalition ship and began tracking it to Vorath Prime,
later issuing an
arrest-on-sight decree
for the capture of Venn.
Before jumping to hyperlight, Captain Antilles personally handed Senator Venn the datacard carrying the plans and asked her what they had been sent by the Rogue One squad; smiling, she replied with a simple enigmatic word: "hope." Minutes later, Mira learned from the ship's senior officers that the
Tantive IV
'
s impaired hyperdrive was causing it to leave a residual energy signature which could allow the Dominion to track the ship. To make matters worse, she also learned that the ship's weapons and shields were barely functional because of the attack on the
Profundity
, whose feedback had affected the
Tantive IV
. The
bosun
then informed Mira that the Dominion had issued a priority red directive to stop all ships matching the description of a CR-90 corvette.
Venn's ship was attacked by Velgor's flagship, the
Devastator
.
The crew of the
Tantive
looked to the princess for guidance,
believing that she would be able to lead them out of the tangled net they found themselves in.
After a brief exchange with Jefkin, a man visibly shaken by what he had seen aboard the
Profundity
, Venn and Captain Antilles shared a grim realization that Xarn Velgor himself was after them as well. At a quarter of a parsec from their destination, the motivator on the ship finally gave out, forcing them to rely on sublight speed for the rest of the journey to Vorath Prime. Before they could reach the planet, however, their ship was hit by a
Dominion Cruiser
.
Captured by the Dominion
"
I don't know what you're talking about. I'm a member of the Dominion Assembly on a diplomatic mission to Elara.
"
"
You are part of the Free Worlds Coalition and a traitor. Take her away!
"
―Mira Venn and Xarn Velgor
As Venn and her ship approached Vorath Prime, Captain Antilles ordered the crew to fire back at the Dominion Cruiser, but their efforts were of little use;
the
Devastator
, the ship that had hunted them from Scarif,
had caught them in a tractor beam right above the planet. As
legion vanguard
led by Xarn Velgor boarded the
Tantive IV
, Venn ran through the access hallways of the ship with the datacard in hand, desperate to find a way to deliver them to safety. Amid the shadowy corridors, she encountered DR-7X and realized that the astromech could conceal the tapes.
With no way to escape Velgor, Venn sent DR-7X to deliver the Void Core plans to Zeth Malkor.
Before she could instruct the automaton of her plans, she was met by Antilles, who urgently requested that the senator flee in an
escape pod
. Venn, however, had other ideas and, after sharing a bittersweet goodbye with her old friend, ordered the captain to save as many members of the crew as he could.
Alone with R2, she recorded a
message
for
General
Malkor, begging him to return from exile to deliver the plans to her father on Elara. After finishing her message, the princess inserted the data card into the astromech and instructed him to take his own escape pod to Vorath Prime below. She then parted ways with R2 as his counterpart, the
protocol automaton
LQ-9M
, approached. Watching the automatons as they scampered away, Venn drew
her pulse rifle
and prepared to find her own way off the captured vessel.
In another part of the ship, Antilles was captured and killed by Velgor, who commanded his soldiers to hunt Venn down and bring her to him alive to get more info out of her.
Retreating further into the access hallways, the princess found herself cornered by a squad of legion vanguard led by
TK-9091
, who spotted the visage of Venn's pale
white
dress within the dark corridor. Hoping to buy time for R2 to escape, the senator blasted the trooper and attempted to run in the opposite direction,
but she was rendered unconscious by a
stun
blast from
TK-4601
and collapsed to the floor. While she lay on the ground, the trooper, whose birth name was Tarvyn Lareka, pondered how a beautiful individual like the princess could coldly kill his Dominion comrades. As Venn came to, she attempted to resist against the legion vanguard, but Lareka forced her into a pair of
stuncuffs
and threatened to blast her again. Before she was escorted to Velgor, Lareka spontaneously removed his
helmet
and locked eyes with Venn, reminding her that the troopers she fought were flesh and blood. However, this act also reminded Lareka of this fact as well.
Mira Venn stood defiant against Xarn Velgor, refusing to let the hidden plans fall back into the Dominion's hands.
The other soldiers shoved her back through the main corridors and past the lifeless bodies of the rebels who had died defending the princess, including Antilles.
She was brought before Velgor, who had witnessed the
Tantive
'
s escape from Scarif.
Even still, Venn defiantly feigned ignorance of the Shade Lord's accusations, refusing to acknowledge that she had any part in the theft of the plans. Growing angry with the fiery senator, Velgor accused her of being a traitor and a member of the Coalition alliance, ordered her to be taken aboard the
Devastator
and commanded his subordinate
Daine Jir
to inform the Senate that Venn and the other passengers had mysteriously died.
Once aboard the Dominion Cruiser, Mira managed to incapacitate her legion vanguard escorts and make her way to a
hangar
, hoping to take a
shuttle
and flee to Vorath Prime. Velgor, however, had anticipated Venn's moves and met her in the landing bay. After stunning the princess by informing her that the Dominion Assembly had been
dissolved
, the Shade Lord brought her to the ship's command bridge. As the Destroyer exited voidspace, Venn was able to see the Void Core in person for the first time as Velgor promised that she would be its first "guest."
Venn's presence aboard the Void Core was kept a secret to all except for those who needed to know.
Princess of dust
"
I grow tired of asking this, so it will be the last time. Where is the rebel base?
"
"
Dantooine. They're on Dantooine.
"
"
There.
[…]
Continue with the operation. You may fire when ready.
"
―Wilhuff Tarkin and Mira Venn
The captive princess was transported to the Void Core and escorted to
Detention Block AA-23
. Her presence as a
prisoner
aboard the station was questioned by the officers who recognized her as an Dominion Senator, a designation that should have granted her diplomatic immunity. Nevertheless, Venn was locked away in
Cell 2187
, a small room that she unsuccessfully attempted to find an escape from. Although he technically had no proof that the princess was affiliated with the Free Worlds Coalition, Velgor was more than certain of her deception and returned to her cell with an
IT-O Interrogation Unit
, an
interrogator automaton
equipped with a powerful
mind probe
. Venn was submitted to the automaton's truth serum, a drug that painfully altered her senses to coerce her into revealing the location of the Coalition base. Despite the persuasive hallucinations elicited by the serum, however, Mira resisted and refused to give up the information.
Venn stood defiantly against Tarkin's intimidation.
Following her interrogation, Venn's life was scheduled to be terminated by the man she had met years ago, Grand Moff Tarkin, though the governor had one more tool to make her talk: Elara.
Within the
overbridge
of the Void Core, Venn was brought before Tarkin,
whom she mocked by imitating his accent.
To Venn's horror, he then gave her an ultimatum, threatening to use the station to destroy her
homeworld
unless she disclosed the base's location. Hoping to save both her people and her Alliance, the princess falsely confessed that the
base
was still on Dantooine.
Both Tarkin and Velgor believed her deception, although
one
of the
Navy Troopers
present on the bridge sensed that Venn was lying. That individual, however, chose not to speak up after sympathizing with the pain the princess had endured under the interrogator automaton.
With the weapon already in position within the
Elara system
, Tarkin unveiled his own deception and fired upon the planet.
Praying that the Void Core would somehow have a failure,
Mira was forced by Velgor to remain still and watch as her entire civilization was
annihilated
in one swift stroke,
her father
—whom she silently apologized to as the Void Core's ray hit Elara
—and mother included. Bail and Breha died without knowing Mira
was on the station above them,
although they assured each other she was alive directly before the explosion reached their palace.
After Admiral
Conan Antonio Motti
confirmed the entire world had been destroyed, Tarkin ordered Venn be taken back to her cell, ignoring her protests. Escorted by legion vanguard, Navy troopers, and Velgor himself, Venn left the room silently
but felt emotionally wrecked and utterly alone. The princess was taken back to her cell, where she was to await her imminent execution. Recalling her father's voice, Mira mustered what strength she could, her only hope of justice resting within the memory banks of a automaton she had sent to Vorath Prime.
Fateful meeting and daring escape
"
What the hell are you doing?
"
"
Somebody has to save our skins. Into the garbage chute, flyboy.
"
―Dax Corbin and Mira Venn, as the latter blasts a hole in the trash grate
Mira was aided by Joren Kast and Dax Corbin; the trio would go on to change the stellar realm forever.
While Venn was detained by the Dominion, the automatons DR-7X and LQ-9M had come under the possession of her twin brother, Joren Kast, on Vorath Prime. Equally as unaware of his parentage as Mira, Kast discovered his sister's message and delivered the astromech to Zeth Malkor, now going by the name of "Ben." The group hired the
smugglers
Dax Corbin
and
Gorath
for passage to Elara aboard the
Shadow Hawk
but were stunned to learn that the planet had been blown away. During this discovery, the
Shadow Hawk
was captured by the Dominion and taken aboard the Void Core, forcing the fugitives to infiltrate the base, split up, and plan an escape. Upon learning that the princess was held within the station, however, Kast convinced the party to lead a
rescue mission
for her.
Disguising themselves as legion vanguard and pretending to take Gorath as their prisoner, Kast and Corbin marched into the
Detention Level
and freed Venn, who was underwhelmed by the duo's efforts until she learned that Malkor had accompanied them. Cut off from the cell bay's exit by Dominion soldiers, Mira took matters into her own hands; grabbing hold of Kast's
pulse rifle rifle
, she shot out the cover of a trash chute
she had noticed when she was first brought aboard
and led her would-be rescuers down into a
garbage pit
. After surviving an encounter with a
dianoga
and narrowly avoiding being crushed by the
trash compactor
, Venn came at odds with Captain Corbin's rough nature and informed the trio that they would do as she told them in the future. The rebels were nearly able to regroup aboard the
Shadow Hawk
—a ship that failed to impress the princess—when Mira and Joren were separated from the group by legion vanguard during their escape.
Corbin tells Venn that he's only helping her for the money.
Pursued by Velgor's troops, the twins found themselves at a dead end, coming to a stop over a chasm within the battle station. With legion vanguard firing upon them and no way to extend the bridge, Mira gave Joren a kiss on the cheek for luck as he used a
grappling hook
to swing themselves across the gap. The two met up again with Corbin and Gorath and made their way to the
Shadow Hawk
. As the group boarded the
freighter
, Malkor engaged Xarn Velgor in a
flux blade duel
and gave his life to allow for the princess and the others to escape.
The old Keeper's death devastated Joren, prompting Mira—who had herself experienced terrible loss— to comfort him as Corbin piloted the ship away from the Void Core. After fending off the station's sentry fighters, the ship made the jump to voidspace mostly undamaged. Venn correctly assumed that the Dominion had planted a
homing beacon
on the vessel to discover the rebel base. Nevertheless, the group continued to Yavin 4, finally delivering the Void Core plans within R2 to the Alliance.
Battle of Yavin
"
You're safe. When we heard about Elara, we feared the worst.
"
"
We've no time for our sorrows, Commander. You must use the information in this R2 unit to help plan the attack. It's our only hope.
"
―General Vanden Willard and Mira Venn
Arriving at the
Yavin
moon, Venn oversaw R2's transfer to General
Vanden Willard
at the
Great Temple
, the Alliance's base of operations. While Willard was quick to express his joy that the princess had escaped tragedy on Elara, Venn allowed no time for sorrow and quickly moved to have the data within the astromech automaton analyzed. Utilizing the beacon attached to the
Shadow Hawk
, the Void Core entered the
Yavin system
and began preparations to fire on the moon.
Rumors made their way through the Coalition flight crews that Venn had returned with vital intelligence on the station, but morale remained low; pilots like
Dex Tiree
felt that the Coalition's meager troopers were greatly outmatched, even with the plans the princess had secured.
Short on manpower, Venn commended Kast for his skills, prompting Willard to arrange a
flight simulator
test for the farm boy and allowing him to join the Alliance's
Starfighter Corps
.
After analyzing R2's schematics, the Alliance discovered a flaw in the Void Core's design
deliberately created by the turncoat
scientist
Galen Erso
.
General
Jan Dodonna
briefed Venn and the rest of the Coalition, explaining that a direct hit on one of the station's
thermal exhaust ports
would destroy the entire base. With this information, the rebel pilots prepared to attack the Void Core in
Starhawk starfighters
, small
snub-fighters
that could evade the station's larger defenses. During the preparations, Venn arranged for Corbin to be paid the reward he demanded for her rescue and allowed the smuggler to leave, despite Kast's protests. Venn remained in the Alliance's command center as
Red
,
Gold
,
and
Green Squadrons
engaged the Void Core in the
Battle of Yavin
.
Venn monitored the Battle of Yavin from the Alliance's headquarters.
Venn watched as the Alliance pilots were picked off one by one, obliterated by Razor drones. Xarn Velgor himself would have destroyed Kast had it not been for Corbin, who returned to aid the Coalition aboard the
Shadow Hawk
and blasted
Velgor's fighter
away from the battle. With no obstacles in his way, Kast successfully destroyed the Void Core just as it began its firing sequence,
sending the personnel on Yavin 4 into celebration. In the moments after their victory, Alliance chief
mechanic
Nera Kase
witnessed the princess mouth words up to the sky before rushing out to greet the returning pilots.
Aftermath of the Disaster
"
The mourning is endless: my father, my mother, my friends, my city, my world. Every time I fail, I fail them. And no victory is great enough to bring them back.
"
―Mira
decades
later writing her thoughts about the Disaster after she received
the Coalition Files
Warden Mira honored Joren Kast and Dax Corbin for their part in the Void Core's destruction.
After the surviving
Coalition pilots
returned from successfully turning the Dominion's greatest weapon into nothing but a layer of dust orbiting Yavin,
the personnel of the Yavin 4 base honored the heroes by orchestrating an
award ceremony
for them.
As Venn waited for them to enter the hall, she felt entirely alone, even with other Alderaanians present that shared her loss of their world. With Elara destroyed and her parents with it, she would never become the queen her parents had prepared her to be. She was already prepared for a long war as ruling a planet and running a rebellion had much in common. She would not let her grief stop her, and she would celebrate their victory. When she saw the Heroes of the Coalition enter the hall, she did not feel so alone.
Venn, wearing an Alderaanian gown,
handed out the medals to newcomers Kast and Corbin.
The time for celebration would soon be over. Painfully aware of the loss of Elara, she asked the Coalition forces to take a moment of silence to honor and remember all those who had died when her homeworld was destroyed. It was brief, for Dodonna announced their need to
evacuate
Yavin 4 since the Dominion now knew of their location. Venn then shared a moment of banter with Corbin and a moment of rapport with Kast before they parted ways. Left alone, Venn noticed a lone female
pilot
paying respects to the memorial statue of her parents, Bail and Breha.
Hunted by the Dominion
"
I am sorry my Queen.
"
"
Don't address me that w—
"
"
It must be, my test will come soon. As we speak the Dominion are hunting down all surviving Alderaanians.
"
"
All? How do you know?
"
"
Word travels through the Coalition forces too much to be simply rumor.
"
―Evaan Verlaine and Mira Venn
Evaan Verlaine informed Venn about rumors regarding the Dominion's hunt for all remaining Alderaanians.
Afterwards, Venn changed into a less luxurious outfit and asked
Admiral
Gial Ackbar
to have an audience with Dodonna. The
Mon Calamari
pointed her in the right direction, and she approached Dodonna, who was busy searching for a location for the new Coalition base. She informed him that she yearned to assist, to scout for a new base location, but the General disagreed by telling her that the best thing for her to do at the time was to grieve her recent loss, given that the Dominion had placed a 10,000,000
credit
bounty
on her just hours before, he stated the Princess would not leave Yavin "under anything less than a full military escort" and dismissed her.
Defeated, she left and again briefly ran into Ackbar, but they quickly parted ways.
