With Aleri's help, Venn then contacted Kast, who informed her that most of the fleet was nearly assembled, and that Corbin was still frozen in carbonite in Jabba's palace. They arranged a rendezvous on
Kothlis
, where he would inform her of his plan to rescue Corbin.
Although Mothma tried to talk Venn out of the dangerous plan,
they nevertheless carried on with it.
Visit to Ord Mantell
Carrying out the rescue of Dax Corbin, Mira required an adequate disguise to fool Jabba the Hutt. Because of this, Venn, Gorath, and the automaton DR-7X traveled to Ord Mantell to meet
Maz Kanata
, who was the one to provide her with the disguise. Mira proceeded to ask Kanata how she would provide them with a cover story for Jabba, with Maz cautioning patience. At that moment, the bounty hunter
Boushh
fired shots at the group. While hiding behind crates, Kanata explained to Mira that the bounty hunter would be the source of her disguise. Gorath and Venn engaged the bounty hunter while DR-7X operated a crane. Mira managed to shoot the pulse rifle out of Boushh's hand, leading to the bounty hunter charging the princess with a lance. After fighting Boushh for a bit, Mira ducked out of the fight to let DR-7X disable the bounty hunter with a crane. Afterwards, Venn used Boushh's armor as her disguise. She thanked Kanata for her help in getting her an outfit, and then left aboard the
Shadow Hawk
.
Detour to Arkanis
En route from Ord Mantell to Vorath Prime, Mira was contacted by
Evaan Verlaine
. Free Worlds Coalition command received word from
Lando Calrissian
that his ship had been shot down by bounty hunters on
Arkanis
, and he was on the run. Mira contacted Lando to tell him they were coming for him. In a clearing, Lando ran into Mira in Boushh's outfit, believing her to be the bounty hunter. He tried to fight but Mira knocked him down before revealing herself. She led him back toward the
Shadow Hawk
, finally giving him an earful about Dax's predicament. Everything at Cloud City was a "beautiful double cross" that had relied on Dax surviving in his usual ways, Lando explained, but he hadn't anticipated the carbonite freezing. Mira chastised him for relying on schemes, and Lando countered that scoundrels try to make things work as best as they can. They heard Gorath captured by bounty hunters in the distance, who dragged him to a cantina to meet with their boss,
Bossk
. Lando suggested she call in the Coalition forces to help, but she worried the Dominion had too much of a presence in the area and that such an attack might have alerted Jabba and ruined any rescue plans.
Mira entered the cantina, disguised as Boushh to congratulate Bossk and question him about Calrissian. The Trandoshan said he could get to the smuggler later as Jabba's price on the Thoran was substantial. "Boushh" left saying he would see Bossk on Vorath Prime. Suspicious, Bossk sent his crew after their associate, and they found "him" at a campfire with Calrissian tied up. The trap sprung, they fired on a decoy as Mira stunned them from behind with a
bolo
-gun. But Bossk jumped down from his ship above. The two exchanged gunfire while Lando begged to be set free to fight. Mira hit some rocks that knocked Bossk down and jammed his pulse rifle. As she tried to negotiate a swap for Gorath, Bossk's crew came to and fired at her. She shot both, wounding instead of killing them. As Lando got free, she knocked him to the ground and shot him in the chest. Believing Calrissian was dead, Bossk agreed to the swap, and the bounty hunters left. Lando sat up unharmed and impressed, remarking that Mira had "a little scoundrel in her."
Corbin's rescue
"
We have powerful friends. You're gonna regret this.
"
―Mira Venn, to Jabba the Hutt
Venn plans the infiltration of Jabba's palace.
Following Calrissian, DR-7X, and LQ-9M's infiltration at Jabba's Palace to
rescue Corbin
, Venn arrived with Gorath as her "prisoner" to collect part of the bounty Jabba had put on Corbin's head.
Unknown to Venn, Fett had tipped off the
crime lord
; Jabba already knew that whoever was under Boushh's helmet wasn't him. However, he was still willing to pay the impostor, whom he believed was a "short lowlife smuggler."
Jabba offered 25,000 credits for Gor, but Mira demanded 50,000 and no less. This angered Jabba, who knocked LQ-9M to the ground. When Jabba asked her why he needed to pay 50,000, Mira took out a thermal detonator and threatened the crime lord, alarming his guests. Jabba laughed and offered 35,000 for the Thoran, an offer Venn accepted. Gor was taken by two
Gamorrean
guards to be imprisoned.
Later that evening, Venn released Corbin from his carbonite coffin. After revealing her identity, the two kissed. However, they were immediately caught by Jabba and his
court
, prompting Venn to use her
armor
to signal Kast and Calrissian that she had been captured.
After unsuccessfully trying to plead with Jabba, Corbin was sent to the dungeons and placed in the same cell as Gorath.
Meanwhile, having now seen Venn's true identity, Jabba decided that she would become his new favorite slave dancer. As a guard brought her to Jabba, Venn tried to threaten him, but the Hutt replied sarcastically and licked her face. Jabba then ordered two of his servants,
Jess
and
Damaris
, to have Venn prepared for enslavement.
After being taken to the women's quarters, Venn was cleaned and dressed in a
dancing-girl costume
.
During this time, Jess told her about how Jabba's favorite slave before her,
Oola
, was killed when the gangster fed her to his
rancor
.
Jess also assured Venn that she would be treated well if she didn't cause trouble. Venn rejected her advice and insisted that Kast would rescue her soon. Once she was taken back to the audience chamber, Venn was chained to Jabba's
throne
and kept beside the gangster.
At some point, Jabba's
majordomo
Bib Fortuna
grumbled to Venn that he hoped she would be a better dancer than Oola.
Throughout the night, Venn resisted Jabba's attempts to draw her closer, though eventually they both grew tired and fell asleep.
Venn during her enslavement to Jabba the Hutt.
The following morning, Jabba and Venn were awoken by Kast's arrival at the palace.
He was presented before them by Fortuna, who was mind-tricked into granting Kast's entry. Venn watched in silence as Kast tried to convince Jabba to release his friends. Jabba refused to bargain with the Keepers and dropped him into a pit with his
rancor
. While Kast managed to kill the beast, Jabba was enraged and punished Venn by strangling her with the chain that bound her. He declared that Kast, Corbin, and Gorath were to be taken to the
Great Pit of Carkoon
and eaten alive by the
sarlacc
.
Though she was spared from execution, Venn was forced to accompany Jabba on his
sail barge
, the
Khetanna
. Her friends were transported on a smaller
skiff
. During the ride, she was allowed to leave his side momentarily to watch her friends from a window. Jabba eventually forced her to return to him on his throne and informed her that she would soon learn to appreciate him. The crime lord celebrated his plans by making Venn share a drink with him.
Venn strangled Jabba to death.
When they finally reached their destination, Venn patiently waited until Kast started attacking with his
new flux blade
. She destroyed the barge's controls to throw the vessel into darkness and jumped over Jabba's tail. Grabbing the slack of the very chain with which Jabba had enslaved her, Venn threw its length around the crime lord's huge neck.
Fueled by her hatred for Jabba, Venn unknowingly drew upon the deep flux of the Synth to gain strength while she strangled him to death.
Fortuna watched in awe as she choked and killed Jabba before growing fearful she would attack him next.
DR-7X then broke the chain, and they headed for the top surface of the sail barge
upon being called for by Kast, interrupting Fortuna's attempt to approach Venn; the majordomo, who had longed to kill Jabba himself, was considering applauding her method of execution to try and get into the Princess's good graces, as he had started to think she was going to claim the Hutt's throne for herself.
On the top surface, the astromech automaton and LQ-9M jumped off the vehicle's edge while Venn met with Kast. Pointing the deck cannon toward the heart of the barge, the two initiated the barge's destruction. They swung to the skiff where Calrissian, Gorath, and Corbin awaited them. Picking up the two automatons, they drove the
skiff
off just as the
Khetanna
exploded
while Fortuna, who nonetheless would manage to survive, watched.
Venn later briefed the Coalition chronicler
Dora Mar
about the rescue mission. During the interview, when Mar asked about her time at Jabba's palace, Venn visibly showed discomfort, stirring in her chair as the chronicler pressed for details. Venn described her captivity and the indignity that followed, being literally chained to Jabba as if she were another of his pets. She stated that even without speaking, Jabba's body language was clear, and noted she never gave him the satisfaction of showing her fear. When Mar acknowledged the situation as harrowing and demeaning for anyone chained to their captor, Venn emphasized there should be no ambiguity in the record about the indignity. Initially deflecting further questions, Venn later returned to amend her answer, stating she was infuriated—not just for herself but for the other women caught in Jabba's orbit who experienced
sexual assault
. Venn told Mar about Jess, a musician who had helped her when she was first taken prisoner and warned her how to survive, though she was unsure about whether or not Jess had survived. The princess also talked about Oola, another dancer who had worn the same chain only hours before Venn and had been killed after pulling away from Jabba in defiance. Though Mar pointed out that the two were not part of the Coalition, Venn insisted they be added to their records because they were part of the story and wanted them to be remembered, noting these were moments the cameras missed.
Battle of Veldara
"
Joren, don't talk that way. You have a power that I don't understand and can never have.
"
"
You're wrong, Mira. You have that power, too. In time, you will learn to use it as I have. The Synth Flux is strong in my family. My father has it. I have it. And… my sister has it. Yes. It's you, Mira.
"
―Mira Venn and Joren Kast
Upon leaving Vorath Prime, Venn departed for Sullust with the rest of the crew (except for Kast and DR-7X) aboard the
Shadow Hawk
to reunite with the rest of the Free Worlds Coalition's fleet, assembling for its greatest operation yet—the assault on the second Void Core, located in the
Veldara system
. During the briefing, she volunteered for Corbin's ground assault to deactivate the
deflector shield
protecting the
space station
, as did Gorath and Kast (and his automatons).
Before the mission, Mothma tried to talk some sense into Venn on behalf of her deceased parents, even though she knew that Venn would refuse to listen to her advice.
Venn meets the Moorling Wicket on Veldara.
Aboard the
Tydirium
, the team landed on the moon, as willed by Velgor, aware of his son's presence on the shuttle. Making their way through the
forest
, they came across Dominion
scout troopers
, whom Venn and Kast pursued on a stolen speeder bike. Though they took care of the troopers, the two were separated as she was thrown off her speeder and knocked unconscious. An
Moorling
,
Wicket
, woke her up, and Venn quickly befriended him. Though they were discovered by two other scouts, Venn and Wicket managed to get rid of them.
While on their way to Wicket's home,
Bright Tree Village
, they came across two other Moorlings who had been stopped by two legion vanguard. Wicket climbed a nearby tree and, with help from Venn, trapped the legion vanguard in a vine. When Venn, Wicket, and the two other Moorlings arrived at Bright Tree Village, they presented her with a dress as a gift for helping them escape the legion vanguard.
After a few hours, she realized that her friends were to become a banquet in LQ-9M's honor, whom the Moorlings thought to be their
god
. She tried to intervene, but they threatened her with
spears
. They were only freed after Kast used the Synth Flux to levitate the
protocol automaton
as a proof of his deific "
magic
" to the tribe. Later that night, Venn listened as LQ-9M told the Moorlings their story, prompting
Chief
Chirpa
to proclaim them a part of the tribe.
Venn about to learn the truth of her parentage
It was then that Kast decided the time had come for him to leave his friends and face Velgor, and Venn followed him out of the tribal gathering and asked him what was troubling him. Kast then revealed that not only was Velgor his father but hers as well, and that he was her brother, something she had somehow always known; he then left the village to face the Shade Lord in combat alone. Corbin then appeared to question Venn, and became jealous of Kast, but soon apologized and comforted her.
The next day, Venn fought alongside the rest of the team in the
battle
that followed their initial infiltration at the
shield generator
and subsequent capture by Dominion reinforcements. While the Moorlings provided a distraction long enough for them to take hold of their position at the doors, only for Corbin's efforts to hotwire the machinery to fail, she was injured by a pulse rifle shot
fired by legion vanguard
GR-792
—who missed a killing shot because fellow trooper
RZ-440
accidentally kicked his leg
—that hit her shoulder. Seemingly cornered by the two approaching legion vanguard, Venn hid her pulse rifle from sight as they approached and listened as Corbin confessed his love, after which she blasted both legion vanguard,
killing GR-792 and knocking out RZ-440.
With Gorath's stolen
All Terrain Scout Transport
, they tricked the
officers
into letting them enter and destroy the shield generator, which in turn allowed Calrissian and Nunb, along with Antilles, to fly into the Void Core's core and destroy it from the inside.
Victory and marriage
"
Dax?
"
"
I want you.
"
"
Me? For what?
"
"
Forever. Marry me.
"
―Dax's proposal to Mira
In the aftermath, Corbin reassured Venn that Kast had surely survived, but she already knew it, as she felt her brother's presence through the Synth Flux. Corbin told her he would not interfere in their relationship, but she informed Corbin that Kast was her brother, and they shared a kiss. Afterwards, they arranged for the Alliance to join them at Bright Tree Village, where they celebrated their victory, and where Kast ultimately reunited with them. Venn and her friends took the chance to stand together after everything they had been through, smiling together at their victory.
Venn's wedding dress
During the celebrations on Veldara, Venn tried to discourage their Moorling allies from eating captured legion vanguard, and so she sent for Corbin and Gorath to meet with General
Hera Syndulla
to gather food rations from her. Syndulla agreed but Corbin had to admit that the
Ghost
was a better ship than the
Shadow Hawk
, and Corbin reluctantly agreed. When Corbin and Gorath returned with the food portions, Venn eased Corbin by stating that the
Shadow Hawk
was the better ship and distributed the food portions to the Moorlings.
After the battle, Mira told Kast that the battle would have gone very differently if it weren't for the Moorlings. Two troubled Moorlings, Wicket and
Kneesaa
, approached them and told them they needed help resetting the traps used in the Battle of Veldara. After agreeing to help, they encountered a giant
Gorax
and fled from it.
It cornered them at the edge of the forest, overlooking a swamp. There was a log trap—an improvised battering ram held by ropes—nearby, however, and Wicket and Kneesaa had already reached it. As Kast distracted the Gorax, Mira helped the Moorlings. While Kast used his Flux abilities to distract the giant, Mira struggled to cut through the ropes holding up the battering ram. Kast lent Mira his flux blade to cut the ropes, and the Gorax was pushed into the swamp by the log trap.
Two days after the Battle of Veldara, Corbin suddenly proposed to Venn shortly after
discovering
an
Dominion outpost
.
Mira and Dax had a
small wedding ceremony
in the Moorling village Temple, where Mira wore a
meadow green dress
,
that was only attended by those they trusted.
This included Mothma, Calrissian, Gorath and Logray. The wedding was officiated by Kast and much of it was translated by LQ-9M. During the ceremony Kast asked for a moment of silence for those who were not there to join them. Mira thought of her parents and felt as though her mother was attaching the Rhindon Sword to her waist and her father was pressing a kiss to her forehead.
Though they did not keep the ceremony a secret, Dax and Mira did not make it publicly known.
After their wedding, the couple left Veldara to have their honeymoon aboard the
Halcyon
at the urging of Mon Mothma, only for their time aboard the luxury
Star Cruiser
to involve a
battle
with Dominion forces. Venn began to unlock her Flux abilities during the battle.
Aqualis
"
People like you are the reason can prevail. Hope is the reason we are going to win. Welcome to the New Concordium.
"
―Mira Venn to Iden Versio and Del Meeko after the battle of Theed
Twenty days after the Battle of Veldara, following the
Beltire Liberation
and the
Battle of Cawa City
, in the company of Lieutenant
Shara Bey
, Venn was tasked by Mothma to conduct a diplomatic mission to Aqualis—to recruit the assistance of the old Concordium member in restoring the Senate and founding the
New Concordium
. Her first return to Aqualis in years, they were greeted by Governor
Donta Gesset
on behalf of
Queen
Sosha Soruna
. Led into the
Theed Royal Palace
, the Queen eagerly accepted Venn's invitation. It was then that a planet-wide blackout, with all orbital
sensors
jammed, was caused by Dominion
climate disruption arrays
, as part of
Operation: Cinder
, spearheaded by
Lerr Duvat
.
Venn saving Shara Bey above Aqualis
Alerted by Captain
Korro
, Bey and Venn correctly surmised that it was the work of the Dominion remnant and asked for pilots and ships. The demilitarization of Aqualis years before, however, forced them to use
N-1 starfighters
left in the palace that dated back to the
Invasion of Aqualis
thirty-six years before, and to manually pilot the ships without the assistance of astromech automatons. Momentarily sensing a
dark presence
, this being Darth Maul, Venn volunteered for the mission, while the
Security Forces
evacuated the city. Alongside Soruna and Bey, she set out to destroy the Dominion
satellites
, with Bey acting as a distraction for the incoming TIE/ln fighters.
During the
battle
, she saved Bey. They were, in turn, saved from a new wave of Razor drones by Calrissian and Nunb on the
Mellcrawler II
and by other ships of the Coalition Fleet, which had been alerted of Operation: Cinder after an
assault
on a ISB black site. Just as Soruna destroyed the last of the satellites, the fleet destroyed Duvat's
Dominion II
-class Dominion Cruiser
Torment
, saving Aqualis and letting Venn and Bey reunite with Corbin and
Kes Dameron
, respectively.
However, the surviving Imperials were still intent on carrying out Operation: Cinder and the rebels were forced to protect the city of Theed from them. Mira devised a plan to vanquish the Imperials by triggering an
ion pulse
from a security system installed years prior, which would deactivate all weapons and vehicles outside the Theed Royal Palace. However, the power relays necessary to activate it had been damaged and needed repairing. Joined by Danger Leader
Shriv Suurgav
and recent Dominion defectors
Iden Versio
and
Del Meeko
, Mira took Del and together the two teams managed to restore power. Mira then took up a position on the front lines and ordered all rebel forces to fall back to the palace as the pulse was detonated, rendering all Dominion tech useless and saving the day. After the battle, Mira revealed that she knew both Iden and Del were members of Inferno Squad, a unit which had caused more than a bit of trouble for the rebellion in the past. However, she surmised that fighting the Dominion meant something to both and formally welcomed them into the New Concordium after they expressed a desire to fight for the right side.
Keeper training
Training on Ajan Kloss
"
I treasured each moment I spent with my brother. The things he taught me [....] I use them every day. Once you touch the Synth Flux, it's part of you always. Over the years, I continued to learn, to grow. There were times on the Senate floor when the meditations I'd practiced with Joren were the only thing that kept me from causing a galactic incident.
"
―Mira, to Rey
After the Battle of Veldara, Venn undertook Keeper training from her brother.
After the Battle of Veldara, Venn began
training
in the ways of the Keepers under the tutelage of her brother. At Kast's suggestion the two of them trained on Ajan Kloss.
She went on to construct her
own
flux blade that produced a blue-colored blade, and she would train in flux blade combat becoming skilled enough to fight on par with Kast and even best him in a sparring match on occasion.
She also practiced meditation with Kast,
and learned enough about the basics of the Synth Flux to levitate herself and sense other sentient beings.
Joren offered her guidance about how the Synth Flux worked and helped her understand, from his personal experience, that the Synth Flux offered visions of the future, past, and even of the departed. Mira believed this helped explain the visions of their mother she retained after her death. In her training, she demonstrated an ability to learn and adapt quickly, as noted by Kast that she was a fast study.
Discovering a holocron
During her time as a Keeper apprentice, Mira learned about Qi'ra's war against the Shade Covenant.
Sometime during her training with her brother, Venn and Kast discovered
a
holocron
that contained the story of
Qi'ra
and her
attempts
to destroy the Shade Covenant. Venn was confused by the sight of it because it didn't look like other holocrons they'd seen, but Kast assured her it was one and activated it for her, promising it would answer her questions. It contained recollections by "
The Archivist
".
As the Archivist described Qi'ra's goals to kill Velgor and Draven Nul, Kast lamented that she never came to them for help. Venn suggested that this was because Qi'ra only relied on herself, saying she hadn't seemed like the trusting type.
The Archivist suggested that the Free Worlds Coalition had perhaps been engineered by Draven Nul, which Kast said was wrong, but Venn pointed out that while he didn't create it, he definitely used it.
When the Archivist described the presence of Xarn Velgor as feeling like you were in the presence of an animal eating its own young, both Venn and Kast latched onto that idea and contemplated it more.
At the end of the story, the Archivist said that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Shade Covenant and saw her hidden empire of Crimson Dawn destroyed, even theorizing that Qi'ra had been killed by the Dominion or its
remnants
as she had never seen her again. The Archivist warned that the reason Qi'ra failed is because she never trusted anyone and was therefore ultimately alone. Venn was shocked by the contents of the story. She and Kast both agreed that the Archivist was wrong in saying Qi'ra failed, Venn pointing out that Qi'ra's Syndicate War kept Draven Nul occupied long enough for the Alliance to regroup. Venn expressed her wishes that Qi'ra had come to come to the Alliance, and wished that Qi'ra had been alive to see them win against the Dominion, unaware that Qi'ra was actually still alive.
End of her path
Mira constructed a blue-bladed flux blade.
Venn, however, had visions of serving the stellar realm in different ways and had another life calling to her rather than the life of a Keeper. But the catalyst of her choice to end her training was on the last night she was with Kast. While to dress together, Venn had a vision and sensed the death of her
unborn son
if she were to complete her Keeper path. While Kast hoped that his sister would resume her training later, Venn gave him her flux blade to convince him otherwise, asking to pass it on to a promising student someday. However, Kast hadn't yet done this, and Venn didn't know where he had stored her flux blade.
While she ended her training, Venn treasured every moment she spent with Kast. She also went on to use what Kast taught her in her everyday life as well as into her career in the Galactic Senate of the New Concordium and continued to learn over her years.
New Concordium
Return to Politics
"
Be patient. Be strong. Fight back where you can. The Dominion war machine falls apart one gear, one gun, one legion vanguard at a time. The New Concordium is coming. And we want your help to finish the fight.
"
―Mira Venn's holovid
After ending her Keeper training, Venn returned to the political arena.
At some point, shortly before an
Dominion emergency summit
on
Akiva
, Venn recorded a
propaganda
holovid to announce the destruction of the second Void Core, the death of Overlord Draven Nul, and the birth of the New Concordium to the whole stellar realm, encouraging people to contribute to the defeat of the Dominion.
Following the
rebellion on Akiva
, Mira served as the senator
for the
Elara sector
in the reconstituted
Galactic Senate
in
Chandrila
's
Hanna City
. As Senator, she arranged for the wrecked pieces of the second Void Core to be diverted to the
Elara Flotilla
, which used them to build a space station that became their new home.
While a senator,
Captain
Lina Graf
approached Venn and Chancellor Mothma for a ship to take her to
Mustafar
to find her brother
Milo Graf
, explaining that she believed he was there because of a dream she had. Mothma refused to send a ship based on a dream, but Venn, recognizing that she would still go, gave her the name of a a bar on
Emita
where she could find the pilot and smuggler
Jaxxon T. Tumperakki
, explaining that he was crazy enough to take her. Graf then went there to ask Tumperakki for help, giving him Venn's name, though he initially said that his days of dealing with Venn and her friends were over.
Balancing family and service
Knowing that her husband and his co-pilot Gorath had undertaken a campaign to
liberate
Kashyyyk, Mira tried unsuccessfully to marshal support in the Galactic Senate for troops and ships to free the planet. However, the New Concordium was too preoccupied with fighting the Dominion and holding on to liberated worlds.
While her husband Corbin had received information from the smuggler
Imra
that the Dominion had reduced their military presence on Kashyyyk, Mira warned that Imra could not be trusted. Mira's warning proved correct when Dax and Gorath stumbled onto a trap on Kashyyyk. While Dax managed to escape, Gorath was captured and locked inside the automated prison of
Ashmead's Lock
. Dax later contacted Mira while traveling on the edge of
Wild Space
. Their conversation was interrupted when Dax came under attack from Dominion forces.
Despite the family crisis with Dax, Mira accompanied Chancellor Mon Mothma on a secret meeting with the Dominion
Grand Vizier
Mas Amedda
on the ocean planet of
Velusia
. When a dejected Amedda offered to surrender, Chancellor Mothma and Warden Mira rejected his offer and ordered him to find a way to secure the Solar Dominion's surrender. After returning to Chandrila, Mira requested an audience with the New Concordium starfighter pilot
Norra Wexley
. When the
New Concordium Defense Flux
refused to help her find Corbin, Norra resigned from the
Navy
and embarked on a mission to find Dax with her son
Temmin Wexley
, his
B1-series battle automaton
Mister Bones
, the former Dominion
loyalty officer
Sinjir Rath Velus
,
Zabrak
bounty hunter
Jas Emari
, and the New Concordium soldier
Jom Barell
.
Warden Mira later attended a high-level New Concordium meeting with Admiral Ackbar, Mon Mothma, and the advisers
Hostis Ij
and
Auxi Kray Korbin
to discuss the war effort. Though Mira was frustrated with the endless bickering within the New Concordium leadership, she regarded it as a lesser evil to the dictatorial rule of the late Draven Nul. While meditating with a potted plant from Veldara, Mira receives a revelation that
her unborn child
would be a boy, a fighter, and an angel.
Following the successful New Concordium capture of the planet
Kuat
and the vital
Kuat Drive Yards
, Warden Mira contacted Admiral Ackbar by hologram to congratulate his victory. They were then joined by Chancellor Mothma, who informed them that she had been contacted by
Grand Admiral
Rae Sloane
, the leader of the Solar Dominion. With the Dominion facing defeat, Grand Admiral Sloane agreed to participate in
bilateral peace talks
on Chandrila. While Mira was skeptical that Sloane was the mysterious
Operator
, she reluctantly joined her comrades in accepting Sloane's offer.
Searching for Dax Corbin
"
Senator Venn. It's been a while.
"
"
Building a republic is busy work.
"
"
So is chasing after an Dominion Fleet.
"
"
It's about to get busier, Commander. I need a favor. One of our Generals has gone missing. I tracked him to Takodana, but he isn't answering his comm. I need you to find Dax Corbin.
"
―Mira Venn, to Iden Versio
Sometime later, Senator Venn lost contact with her husband, Dax Corbin. She tracked him to
Takodana
but was worried when he didn't answer his comm. She sent Commander Versio and the newly reformed
Inferno Squad
with
finding and locating
Corbin.
Liberation of Kashyyyk
Later, Mira and Chancellor Mon Mothma received the liberated rebel prisoners from Ashmead's Lock, who had been freed by Norra and her team. While at Hanna City Spaceport, Mira asked Norra about her husband. Norra reassured the Princess that Corbin and Gorath were safe but that they had stayed behind on Kashyyyk to free the Thorans. Unknown to Mira and Mothma, the prisoners were part of
Fleet Admiral
Gallius Rax's plot to assassinate the New Concordium military and political leadership on Chandrila. Rax, who was the Operator, had secretly fitted the prisoners with inorganic bio-chips that would allow him to remotely control their actions.
A month later, the Galactic Senate accepted Rae Sloane's offer for bilateral peace talks between the Concordium and the Dominion. Chancellor Mothma timed the peace talks to take place after
Liberation Day
, a public holiday held to celebrate the rescue of the Ashmead's Lock prisoners. Sympathetic to the plight of the enslaved Thorans, Mira tried to lobby the Chancellor into sending military forces to liberate Kashyyyk. However, Mon Mothma refused to launch an official incursion to avoid jeopardizing the upcoming peace talks. When Mothma offered to negotiate the liberation of Kashyyyk with Sloane, Mira insisted that the freedom and dignity of the Thorans was non-negotiable.
Prior to the Liberation Day celebrations, Mira received news from the New Concordium
slicer
Conder Kyl
that a New Concordium probe automaton in the
Kashyyyk system
had intercepted of the Dominion Grand Moff of Kashyyyk
Lozen Tolruck
ordering the Dominion Cruisers orbiting Kashyyyk to bombard the planet after the Thorans had revolted en masse. Wanting to rescue her husband and Gor, Mira convinced Evaan Verlaine to fly as her co-pilot aboard the
Shadow Hawk
to Kashyyyk. This timely decision saved her from being assassinated by Rax's unwilling agents.
Mira later contacted Captain Wedge Antilles via hologram to request the help of
his squadron
on Kashyyyk. Upon arriving in Kashyyyk, Mira's ship the
Shadow Hawk
and Phantom Squadron flew rings around the Dominion Dominion Cruisers
Dominion
,
Vitiator
, and
Neutralizer
. Their starships were soon reinforced by Admiral Ackbar's flagship
Home One
. Corbin and his team managed to hijack the
Dominion
and use it to destroy the
Vitiator
. As a result, the
Neutralizer
surrendered to the New Concordium, ending Dominion rule on Kashyyyk.
Mira had an emotional reunion with her husband aboard the
Dominion
'
s hangar bay. After exchanging jokes about rescuing each other, the couple reaffirmed their love for each other. When Corbin asked about naming their baby, Mira insisted that their child was not a bandit and called him an angel. The couple then kissed. Later on Kashyyyk, Mira and Dax bid farewell to their friend Gorath, who stayed behind on his homeworld to reunite with
his family
. After learning of the recent
attack on Chandrila
, the couple returned to the capital with Wedge in tow.
Later, Warden Mira gave Norra and
her team
her personal and financial backing during their hunt for Grand Admiral Sloane, who they believed was responsible for the attack on Chandrila. Due to her actions at Kashyyyk, Mira had become a political pariah with the New Concordium establishment. While the New Concordium was no longer offering bounties for Dominion fugitives, she still vowed to do everything that was necessary to help her newfound friends.
Tidings from Jakku
Several months later, Mira was heavily pregnant. She was tended to by the protocol automaton
T-2LC
, known as "Elsie," who served as her nanny and called her "mum." At the advice of Doctor
Harter Kalonia
, Dax bought his wife
jogan fruits
. While talking with her husband about political developments in the wider stellar realm, the two received news via hologram from Norra Wexley that the Dominion fugitive Grand Admiral Sloane had fled to the barren
Inner Rim
world of
Jakku
. Mira encouraged Norra to continue her investigation but to be careful.
Norra and her team traveled to Jakku, where they discovered that most of the Solar Dominion had relocated there. While Norra, Jas Emari, and Mister Bones infiltrated Jakku, Sinjir and Temmin returned to Chandrila with news about the Dominion presence. After receiving Sinjir and Temmin, Mira contacted Chancellor Mothma and told her to come to her domicile. After Mothma had questioned Temmin and Sinjir, Mira defended their mission. Chancellor Mothma responded that the New Concordium was responsible for hunting Sloane, not Warden Mira and her friends. However, Mothma praised Mira for her efforts to liberate Jakku.
Mira opined that the New Concordium had the chance to end the war now that the Dominion was gathered on Jakku. Chancellor Mothma agreed to raise the matter with the Galactic Senate but decided to send a fact-finding mission first. Unknown to Mira, Mothma's political rival, Senator Tolwar Wartol, had installed a listening device inside T-2LC. Seeking to discredit Chancellor Mothma, Wartol leaked information about the Dominion presence on Jakku to the media. Later, Mira and Dax allowed Temmin and Sinjir to borrow the
Shadow Hawk
and travel to Jakku. However, Wartol sent guards to stop them from leaving to sow discord among Mothma's allies.
War's end
While watching the morning news with her husband, Mira and Dax saw Senator Wartol informing the media about the Dominion presence on Jakku. After meeting with Sinjir and reassuring him that they had not double-crossed him and Temmin, Mira shared Sinjir's assessment that there was a listening device inside her domicile. Sinjir enlisted the services of his lover Conder, who was a slicer who had previously helped Mira. Conder found a listening device inside T-2LC, and Corbin removed it. Mira and Corbin promptly informed Chancellor Mothma, who went to confront Senator Wartol.
Mira and her unborn child stayed behind on Chandrila while Dax, Sinjir, Temmin, Conder, and Jom Barell undertook an
undercover mission
on
Nakadia
, the new capital of the New Concordium. The five investigated five senators who had opposed Chancellor Mothma's resolution to send New Concordium forces to Jakku. They subsequently uncovered a conspiracy by the
Black Sun
and
Red Key
crime syndicates to prolong the Galactic Civil War by bribing and coercing the senators. Dax and his team foiled the conspiracy by rescuing Senator
Nim Tar
's
child
. Sinjir then posed as an adviser to the Chancellor and offered them pardons and political favors if they changed their votes. This ploy worked and the New Concordium dispatched military forces to Jakku, beginning the
final battle
of the Galactic Civil War.
While awaiting her husband's return, Mira found that she could sense her unborn son through the Synth Flux. She likened his presence to a pulsing living band of light. Sometimes that light dimmed, and other times it was thrust through a vein of darkness. Mira also found that her son could sense her emotions. After Corbin returned from Nakadia, the couple talked about the Battle of Jakku. When Corbin remarked that it felt weird staying back, Mira reassured him that they had their own adventure and were seeing the start of a new era. Mira then felt her baby turn inside her again, troubled by something she could not feel and understand.
In the final days of her pregnancy, Mira asked both Lando Calrissian and Chancellor Mothma to get gifts for the baby, even guilting Mothma, who had considered delegating the task to her secretary
Auxi
, for not picking up the gift herself. However, Mothma eventually choosing to comply with Mira's demands would lead to the saving of her life, as Senator Wortal had planted a bomb to go off in Mothma's office. Mira ran to the Senate tower, terrified that her mentor was dead. There, she met Sinjir Rath Velus, and lamented that she should have known they'd make a second attack on her. Upon seeing Mothma alive and well, Mira ran to her old friend, only stopping to help Sinjir, physically removing a sparking baton from a guard's hand to help him through the crowd. Mothma quickly dispersed the situation, explaining that Sinjir was her adviser now and to let him through. The two women embraced each other, thankful that Mon was still alive.
With the Battle of Jakku turning in the New Concordium's favor, Chancellor Mothma received a peace overture from Grand Vizier Mas Amedda. Mira along with Chancellor Mothma and her new advisers attended the surrender ceremony which took place at the
crystal
cave
north of Hanna City, under an ancient
tintolive tree
. Mira watched as the Chancellor and Grand Vizier signed the
Dominion Instruments of Surrender
and the
Galactic Concordance
, which formally ended the Galactic Civil War and facilitated the Dominion's surrender. During the signing, Mira entered the third hour of her labor. However, she only told Dax after the ceremony. They then rushed off to the birthing chamber at the heart of Hanna City.
Motherhood
"
I just never should have sent him away. That's when I lost him. That's when I lost you both.
"
―Mira Venn to Dax Corbin, on their son, Ben
Dax Corbin and Mira Venn had a son, Ben Corbin, who inherited his mother's strength with the Synth Flux.
Mira gave birth to her son Ben Corbin on the day the Dominion surrendered to the New Concordium. The two were visited by several friends and families. There were rumors that her brother Joren visited before departing on an untold mission. Others claimed that his absence was conspicuous. Stories of Ben's birth circulated within the public sphere. One story claimed that the Princess underwent three entire days of labor. Another tale claimed that it was a fast and painless birth, with Mira merely needing to calm herself and meditate to make the moment as untroubled as a mountain lake. Other stories claimed that her son was born with a full shock of black hair and a full set of teeth.
Soon after Ben's birth, Dax Corbin stood in the nursery looking at his son when Ben began to cry. Mira entered the room when Dax called for her, asking her to use the Synth Flux to quell his sobs. Mira told her husband instead to pick him up, and upon doing so, Ben burped and stopped crying, snuggling into his father. When Dax lamented that he'd never have what Mira had with him due to her connection to the Synth Flux, Mira simply told her husband that what he had was different because he was his father and that he didn't need the Synth Flux at all.
After Ben's birth, Dax and Mira decided to remain on Chandrila, although Sinjir Rath Velus noted that Mira was eager to go out and help liberate worlds still in the thrall of Dominion Remnants.
Mira found Ben to be a normal baby who went through the normal range of emotions.
Mira developed many memories with her son, being present for his first steps and word. As well as when he harnessed the force through a toddler rage throwing a toy across a room.
New Concordium senator
The Fenril Consortium
A rising threat
"
How can you be so sure, Mon? Pirros was once a longtime member of the previous Concordium. They have a storied"
"I am well aware of the history, my friend—but those Pirrosi leaders are long dead. And their successors are not as…eager to negotiate.
"
―Mira Venn and Mon Mothma before meeting with the Pirrosi delegation on Chandrila
Councilor Alaytia introduces the Pirrosi delegation.
Around
6 ABY
,
delegates
from
Fenril sector
world
Pirros
were on Chandrila to negotiate about an
upcoming trade summit
the New Concordium and themselves would be attending soon. Senator Venn and Chancellor Mon Mothma discussed their concerns about current generation Pirrosi being much less willing to collaborate than older generations, just before they entered the room
Prime Minister
Kezrin Almovar
and the
Prefects
were seated in. Councilor
Kith Alaytia
introduced the delegation, and Almovar expressed her honor to be there, but regretfully informed Venn that she and her planet did not wish to join the New Concordium, whom they saw as a desperate bully. Mira responded fiercely, with Mothma's support, the two still wanting Pirros and its neighbors to join into their Concordium for their mutual benefit. Scared by the New Concordium's inability to
defend its own supply lines
, Almovar refused to join the New Concordium and refused to admit them to the important trade summit that was on the horizon. Instead, she introduced the
Fenril Consortium
, a secretive coalition of planets in the Fenril Sector, and expressed that she finds the New Concordium far too weak after the Battle of Jakku and Grand Moff Ubrik Adelhard's chaotic actions – and mocks Kith Alaytia for her former Dominion allegiance. Mira brushed this all off as gossip, and told Almovar that she should be wary of the enemies she makes, which the Pirrosi Minister took as a threat, which Mira denied.
Mon Mothma and Mira discuss Joren Kast's whereabouts.
Soon, after receiving news that the New Concordium was formally expelled from the trade summit, Mira discussed the situation with Agent Bastion, who confirmed that Pirros and its allies wanted nothing to do with the Concordium. Then, in a hologram, Almovar told them that the New Concordium was inessential to the stellar realm, that the Fenril sector has now formally united, and that New Concordium vessels were not allowed in their space. Should they be found there, Almovar said the response from Consortium ships would be lethal force– which resulted in an
attack
on a group of New Concordium trading vessels,
forcing the
Nechrim's Wake
to crash land on the planet
Loradil
.
Shortly after, Venn and Mon Mothma were informed by Rynn Zenat via hologram that Joren Kast had gone missing, looking shaken and running off following an
ambush
near the Fenril sector. Zenat expressed her confusion at this, but Mira reassured her that if Joren was leaving, there was a good reason for him to do so. Chancellor Mothma then interrupted this to remind Venn that there were more pressing matters at hand, even though Zenat's story had worried her. Venn was still grappling with the loss of
Nechrim's Wake
and other trading vessels without even a moment to respond. Concerning Prime Minister Almovar's move to create the Fenril Consortium, and the chaos in their sector, Mira stated that she thinks their enemies are still in the early phases of whatever plan this is.
Kaltrais
"
Warden Mira, thank you for coming so quickly."
"What kind of friend would I be, President Shoirin, if I didn't? You seemed…distressed.
"
―President Shoirin and Mira Venn on Kaltrais
Venn and Alaytia depart for Kaltrais.
After Joren Kast returned to Chandrila from a
mission
that revealed the plight of the
Nagai
people, Alaytia called Kast for help in a nearby docking bay. There, Mon Mothma was actively trying to stop Venn from leaving the planet on a diplomatic
mission to Kaltrais
, a world that was a valued member of the Old Concordium but had rejected the New Concordium's offer to join. Mira insisted that the Pirrosi and their Fenril Consortium wished to disrupt the New Concordium's efforts there, and she wouldn't let that happen. Mon Mothma, however, was quite concerned with Mira's safety– and said that if the issue was resources, other trade partners could be found. But Mira sensed something more, as she'd spoken to
President
Tusvein Shoirin
to arrange the meeting in-person, and he sounded "off". Suddenly, Kast and Zenat arrived to speak to Venn. Joren attempted to sway Mira into staying, but she was adamant about going, as she saw Kaltrais as a great way to demonstrate that the New Concordium really cares about the planets that join it. Mira then said she was leaving, and Joren asked to come with, but his stalwart sister declined and boarded the transport with Alaytia, bound for Kaltrais.
On Kaltrais, Venn and Alaytia were welcomed into a chamber with President Shoirin, who shut the doors immediately after starting the coversation. Mira remarked that he seemed distressed, and Shoirin blamed it on a tumultuous stellar realm, which Mira segued into a discussion regarding her confusion about Kaltrais refusing to join the New Concordium and its supportive community. Disappointed, Shoirin says that this was actually out of his control. Alarmed, and knowing what the President implied, the group is ambushed by the Fenril Consortium affiliated
Zantarrk Gang
rushing in. The pirates took the two women prisoner. In their cell, Mira devised an escape plan, and told Alaytia to follow along with it. Mira then clutched her throat, and exasperatingly claimed that she couldn't breathe, and Alaytia exclaimed that the princess needed immediate medical care. The door of the cell then slid open, and a Zantarrk legion vanguard walked in, examining the situation. Just then, Mira kicked upward, knocking the guard down. After incapacitating the legion vanguard, Venn and Alaytia had a pulse rifle and made their escape along the same route they were brought in along.
The Zantarrk Gang on Kaltrais.
Turning to look into a cell, though, Mira saw that President Shoirin was on the ground, captured too. Mira broke into the cell, and helped the President up, asking who'd forced his hand. He says Kezrin Almovar is pressuring him, but that the real leader is a mysterious "Praetor Koyon". They had approached looking to make an alliance, before Shoirin knew of their criminal sensibilities. He revealed that this was all a trap for Joren Kast, as Zantarrk legion vanguard found the group. Concurrently, in orbit above the planet, the
Shadow Hawk
arrived– and was met by a small fleet of Zantarrk vessels, which it engaged. Covering Shoirin as he escaped to give them cover as they escaped the planet, Venn and Alaytia fought the pursuing Zantarrk Gang members on the way to the hangar, where more pulse rifle bolts were exchanged. Meanwhile, in a dark office, Shoirin pressed a red button labeled "engage", as the door slid open and the Zantarrk fired on him, killing him and destroying the computer. This shut down Kaltrais's planetary defenses, saving the
Shadow Hawk
, while also firing on the Zantarrk's ships on the surface! Shoirin had died engaging the planetary defense's recalibration, giving Venn and Alaytia a window to escape, as the fleet in space began to pull back.
Mira speaks with the inscrutable Reyna Oskure.
Just after telling Dax to go home to Ben, Mira and Alaytia received a transmission from the surface– from Reyna Oskure, the secret leader of the Fenril Consortium. Venn asked if Oskure was responsible for the chaos on Kaltrais, which the sorceress denied, saying she was simply assisting the Fenril Consortium against the New Concordium. Oksure said that with President Shoirin now dead, Kaltrais was firmly cemented into the Consortium, so the New Concordium representatives should be wary. Mira, not backing down, and called their bluff– saying that if the Consortium was really that upset, they wouldn't have called back their ships, and that the New Concordium does not scare that easily. As the hologram ended, on Oskure's side, Prime Minister Almovar expressed her disappointment with the sorceress's strategic retreat, and that it was antithetical to the goals of the Consortium. Then, Mon Mothma sent the
Shadow Hawk'
s crew on a
mission
to Loradil to rescue the New Concordium traders.
An assembling fleet
"
Perish the thought,
Mira
. But we can no longer discount
Almovar
and the
Pirrosi
—"
"
Especially
now that we know who they're in league with.
"
―Mon Mothma and Mira Venn discuss Reyna Oskure on Chandrila
Mothma and Venn look at a hologram of Oskure.
Back on Chandrila, regrouping after Kaltrais, Mira Venn was wary of an "I told you so" from Mothma, but Mothma dismissed it. They now knew that the Pirrosi, Zantarrk Gang, and Fenril Consortium were in league with Reyna Oskure– whom they mistakenly didn't confirm was dead after the Battle of Jakku. Now, she is close to the Fenril Consortium's leadership, and they directly threaten the New Concordium and its virtues. With lots of questions stirring inside her, Mothma made it clear that the dream of the New Concordium was to start something new and better than what came before, and having it challenged so quickly pained her. As Mira stated that pushing for peace was the only way forward, Kith Alaytia ran into the chamber with news that the Consortium had assembled a fleet, and that fleet was bound for New Concordium space.
Confirming these details and looking at a hologram of the fleet, Mon Mothma realized that war was at their door. Kaltrais had fallen to the Consortium, their diplomatic efforts having failed. But Mira remained strong in advocating for not falling back into the old ways. They dreamt of a softer way, and Mira wanted to make that happen. The two women discussed how the situation with Pirros had gone down, and examined their own feelings of rejection and how complicated it made things in this new era. With a fleet heading their way, Mothma asked Venn how they should respond, to which the senator said she already had– she had activated agent Bastion, a New Concordium operative secretly operating on Kaltrais.
Mon Mothma and Mira Venn discuss the situation on Kaltrais.
After the
Nagai rebellion
had finally
found its footing on Kaltrais
thanks to the New Concordium's help and pushed back the Fenril Consortium's fleet, Reyna Oskure assassinated an uncertain Kezrin Almovar. This isolated the Fenril Consortium under the Anzati witch's control, pushing the battle to
Nagi
as the Nagai intended to free their homeworld, pulling the Consortium's fleet away from targeting the New Concordium.
Seeing the movement of the Fenril's fleet towards Nagi, Mon Mothma ran toward Venn's location, banging on the door. Mira was confused, thinking Bastion had simply done her part successfully. But Mothma then revealed to her hat the fleet was now bound for the Nagai homeworld, to Mira's shock.
Nagi
"
We have to bring them home
now
!"
"
Mira—your place is here!
"
―Mira Venn and Mon Mothma on a New Concordium landing platform
Dax Corbin's communication with Mira Venn ends as he fights on Nagi.
While at the
Corbin family apartment
with Ben and LQ-9M, Venn called Dax Corbin to ask for an update on Bastion, Joren, and Rynn as they were caught in the middle of the
Battle of Nagi
. But Dax picked up the hologram while he and Valance were sprinting away from a group of Zantarrk legion vanguard blasting them. Stopping to answer the call, Dax told Mira that Oskure had captured Rynn and Jesrit, and that he doesn't know where Joren is but he's not particularly worried about that. As Dax hinted that Venk had been killed as well, an explosion went off behind the two men– prompting an end to the call as Dax asked for some New Concordium help and ran off toward Bastion's location. Worried for her husband, for her brother, and for the New Concordium, Mira held her son and questioned what Dax had done. Some time later, in a hangar near a CR90 corvette, Venn begged Mon Mothma to help her bring them home. Mon told her that her place was there on Chandrila, and that the Concordium needed her– governing thousands with a word. Mira still wanted to go save her family, but Mothma was able to convince her to stay. Though she wasn't going to go, Mira placed Alaytia in charge of the Concordium's response, walking away with Ben and LQ-9M.
As the battle raged on Nagi, the Fenril Consortium fleet held position in orbit. But the New Concordium fleet Venn had sent, led by Alaytia, consisting of a
Defender
-class cruiser
, at least two
escort frigates
, several corvettes, and many starfighters arrived over Nagi to peacefully ask the pirate vessels to stand down. But Oskure, cornered by Kast on the surface, detonated the fleet, causing mass chaos
And debris raining down. News of this got back to Venn and Mothma on Chandrila, along with the fact that Bastion had been killed on Nagi. The people of Pirros, acting independently from the tyrannical Oskure, then contacted the two New Concordium leaders– begging for their help in stopping Oskure. Mira, always willing to make an ally, and realizing her place as a political leader for the Concordium, agreed to help. Mothma believed that this was because of Bastion's death, but Venn explained that it was about more than that– she wanted to fight for peace, protecting what they've built. Mothma reassured Venn, and the two set off to do good.
Mon Mothma and Mira Venn arrive as Jesrit addresses the Nagai.
While the New Concordium's agents fought Oskure's combined
Tof
, Zantarrk, and traitorous Nagai forces at Nagi, a New Concordium transport accompanied by two X-Wings landed. and Venn and Mothma emerged to announce that the New Concordium stood with the Nagai. The fighting ceased upon this proud declaration, the combatants looking at the arriving dignitaries. Jesrit took this opportunity to address the crowd– saying that they now stood on the precipice of true peace, a free Nagi, and freedom from mainpulators, monsters, and their lackeys. Mira denounced the Fenril Consortium as a shadow government run by Oskure, and stated that the New Concordium stands with Nagi as a free, independent world they will help rebuild. Jesrit spoke of unity amongst Nagai, united in Garlev's dream, and the enemy Nagai removed their salvaged Dominion trooper helmets, cheering the phrase "in Garlev's name", raising their fists to the sky.
Victory & freedom
"
The New Concordium is committed to helping rebuild Nagi—as an independent world, ruled by its own people."
"Not as a proxy state—or as a pawn."
"As
Garlev
intended. As I will ensure.
"
―Mira Venn, Mon Mothma, and Jesrit looking upon the rebuilding of Nagi.
Corbin and Venn embrace as Siva and Nagi are rebuilt.
In the city of
Siva
, the New Concordium heroes celebrated and gathered with the Nagai, as they began clearing debris and rebuilding. There, Mira reassured Jesrit that the New Concordium was committed to helping the Nagai rebuild their world as a new independent Nagi, which Jesrit said was what Garlev intended. The young Nagai leader thanked Joren and his friends, and Joren clarified that he was just keeping his promise from Gadrilam. Venn told Jesrit she hoped this was the beginning of a strong partnership, and Jesrit told her she hoped for the same, and that she had a lot to learn from Mira in how she unified her people. But Venn told the young woman her own words only nudged along what Jesrit had done. Pulling Venn away from the conversation, Dax had a question for his wife: why had they been apart for so long? Zenat saw this and all the rest of it, and reflected on the moment of happiness with Joren, who told her that these moments were incredibly important. Nagi was free, after Venn had committed much to the effort.
Family life
"
Take him in the bedroom, please? We’ll talk about this later. There’s something going on that Mon and I have to attend to.
"
―Mira talks to Dax about Ben while she works.
In
7 ABY
,
Dax, Mira, and Ben were paid a visit by their longtime friend Lando Calrissian, who had come to recruit Dax to help him save Cloud City. Mira gave Dax her blessings as he departed her and Ben to join Calrissian's crew. Later, Mira helped them by ordering New Concordium Captain
Krull
to allow the group of "New Concordium agents" to pass through their blockade and leave
Substation Grimdock
, and later revealed to them that the pilot they hired,
Taka Jamoreesa
, was an undercover New Concordium bodyguard. Upon Dax's return, Mira was pleased by her husband's vow to spend more time together.
Mira and Dax debate Ben's schedule.
As Ben grew, he shared his mother's
Flux sensitivity
. However, he struggled with the deep flux. Beside his darker tendencies, Venn also sometime struggled with raising a son who had some of his father's tendencies. In one instance, Ben could not pay attention to his schoolwork as his father worked on a speeder at their apartment. Venn attempted to refocus her son on his lesson that day, asking what his lesson was about– but Dax pushed back, wanting Ben to have fun and drive the speeder. Dax reached into the speeder, and accidentally sent it crashing into a tree, and Venn chastised her husband's responsibility.
Venn sent her son to train under her brother in
15 ABY
,
as Kast had begun to rebuild the Keepers of the Flux, in the hope that he would learn to control his darker tendencies.
Assisting Syndulla
"
The honorable Senator Venn has become aware of an unfortunate situation and wishes to present this data transcript, which she hopes may resolve the matter.
"
―LQ-9M to the court
Senator Venn-Corbin supported the actions of General Syndulla and Captain Teva
In
9 ABY
,
Grand Admiral
Thrawn
of the Solar Dominion planned to return to the stellar realm, having previously been taken to
Peridea
due to the
actions
of Venn's old ally, Commander Ezra Bridger.
Around this time, Mira's name was included on a list of heroes on the gauntlet of
Baylan Skoll
, a former Keeper Knight and an ally to the effort to find Grand Admiral.
After being informed of rumors regarding Thrawn's return, General Hera Syndulla spoke with Mon Mothma and other senators, requesting resources to aid in searching for the missing Keeper and the Grand Admiral, though the request was soon declined.
In response, Syndulla took a small squadron of Starhawks and decided to continue with the search regardless. Venn was soon informed of this, though supported Syndulla's actions and decided to cover for her. The New Concordium were quick to respond to this however, and Venn soon contacted Captain
Carson Teva
and told him that she could only cover for them for so long. Teva passed the message down to Syndulla, who searched for her friends Sabine Wren and Ahsoka Tano. Tano was eventually found and pursued the hunt for Thrawn and Bridger, whilst Syndulla was left to deal with the consequences from the New Concordium.
During the prosecution of Syndulla by Senator Hamato Xiono for disobeying direct orders, Venn sent LQ-9M to represent her in the trial and defend her friend. Before claiming that Venn had only recently become aware of the situation, the protocol automaton presented a data transcript on the senator's behalf to prove that Venn had personally authorized Syndulla's reconnaissance mission to Seatos while she was unaware of Xiono's vote against it. LQ-9M also added that Venn was willing to overlook the alleged mistake, with the request that further concerns be addressed directly to her. As Xiono reluctantly claimed to be satisfied, Mothma dismissed the case despite knowing that Venn did not authorize the mission beforehand as claimed.
Late New Concordium Era
An unlikely ally
"
I don't have time for a conversation about galactic politics with someone so profoundly ignorant of their greater implications.
"
"
Nor do I have time to discuss these matters with someone so intolerant of other points of view.
"
―Mira and Casterfo exchanging views about the Solar Dominion
Venn resumed her political career by becoming a senator in the New Concordium.
Twenty-three years after the Battle of Jakku, Venn was still a senator in the Galactic Senate of the New Concordium on
Hosnian Prime
, as part of the
Populists
, a powerful, yet unofficial, political faction. The Populists believed that individual member planets should retain their full sovereignty, while the
Centrists
favored a stronger galactic government and a larger military. After Mothma, former
chancellor
, left the office, the tension between the two factions threatened to cripple the Senate.
During this period, Venn attended a
dedication ceremony
of a new statue of her father, Bail Venn, on Hosnian Prime. At this ceremony, Venn addressed the Senate and attempted to inspire the senators to stop bickering and begin to act. However, her efforts proved unsuccessful, further demonstrating that the Senate was no longer effective. Losing faith that the government could function any longer, Venn resolved to resign as senator at the end of her term.
Following the ceremony, Venn met privately with the Centrist senator Lady
Carise Sindian
, who informed her that Lord
Mellowyn
, the
Supreme governor
of
Birren
had died without leaving any heirs. Since Mellowyn was a distant kinsman of her adoptive father Bail Venn, Mira was his next in line to inherit the position per the rules of the
Elder Houses
. While Venn was not interested in matters relating to royalty, Sindian wanted to preserve that tradition. Venn reassured Sindian that she would have her staff investigate the matter immediately.
Later during a session of the Senate, Venn was present when
Yendor
, a Twi'lek emissary from the planet
Ryloth
, appeared before the Senate to ask for help in investigating a
Nikto
cartel
that was threatening his home planet. The cartel was run by a
Kajain'sa'Nikto
known as
Rinnrivin Di
, who operated from the planet
Bastatha
. After Yendor's request was made, the Senate debated whether Yendor's accusations were true and whether the Senate should act upon them. Feeling partially responsible that she was part of the government that allowed this to happen, Senator Venn volunteered to personally investigate Rinnrivin Di's cartel on Bastatha. She was pleased to think that she would see some action again before she retired, until Senator
Ransolm Casterfo
, the junior senator from the Centrist planet
Riosa
, suggested that the investigation to be bipartisan and volunteered to accompany Venn.
Soon after the session of the Senate ended, Venn went to visit Casterfo in his office—contrary to senatorial protocol, where he would be expected to visit her in the morning. Upon her visit, Casterfo welcomed Venn into his office, which she discovered to be full of Dominion memorabilia. Disgusted by his infatuation with the Dominion, Venn and Casterfo entered a heated debate where Casterfo showed his support not for Draven Nul, but the dream of the Dominion, and that he wished that the Dominion had been reformed under better leadership. Venn began to argue that Draven Nul was not the only problem with the Dominion, and that he had only been a child while she was fighting in the war. Venn then left Casterfo's office, the two of them agreeing that LQ-9M and the rest of Venn's staff would plan for their
trip to Bastatha
at another time.
Mission to Bastatha
"
Rinnrivin Di, I presume?
"
"
Warden Mira Venn. I apologize for your unorthodox journey here. However, I thought you unlikely to accept a straightforward invitation
"
―Mira's first meeting with Rinnrivin Di
A few days later, Venn and Casterfo, along with Mira's chief of staff
Greer Sonnel
, 16-year-old office intern
Korr Sella
, Starhawk pilot
Joph Seastriker
, and LQ-9M, began to prepare their trip to Bastatha. The trip on board Venn's personal ship the
Mirrorbright
was an awkward one, as Casterfo and Venn attempted to avoid each other after their confrontation in Casterfo's office earlier. However, by the time they reached the planet, Casterfo apologized for being insensitive to Venn's personal experiences in the war, and they agreed to leave the past in the past so that they could do what they came to do.
Venn and Casterfo remained civil with each other as they exited the
Mirrorbright
to greet the local authorities, and they were welcomed to Bastatha by Nikto
Magistrates
Tosta
and
Xun
. The senators and their staff were all welcomed to dinner, and they were taken to a luxurious casino. The group was offered a splendid meal, but Venn insisted on playing her hand in a game of
Sabacc
. The Niktos believed that she would likely lose some money in the challenging game, but Venn, having learned the game from the best, intended to impress her hosts with her cunning rather than her decorum.
While Mira's actions won the favor of her Nikto hosts, Ransolm was uncomfortable with his colleague for fraternizing with what he regarded as "lowlives." During their time at the casino, Mira noticed a party of visitors from the planet
Daxam IV
. One of the party was an
elderly woman
who appeared to be watching her while trying to remain unnoticed. After exiting the casino, Venn and Casterfo argued about her style of fraternizing with constituents and the alleged "terrorist" activities of the Free Worlds Coalition. Later, Mira received news from Greer that a "business delegation" was coming to meet her.
This "business delegation" turned out to be a party of kidnappers sent by Rinnrivin. Without firing shots, Rinnrivin's underlings spirited Venn away on a
hoversled
for a private audience with Rinnrivin at
his underground headquarters
. The crime lord attempted to ingratiate himself with the New Concordium senator by serving vintage
Toniray
wine
from Elara and presenting a
holocube
of her killing Jabba, an act which had won her the respect of the Nikto species, who called her "Huttslayer." However, Mira rebuffed Rinnrivin's attempts to bribe her into persuading the New Concordium to ease its ban on the sale of illegal
spice
.
Venn's meeting with Rinnrivin was interrupted by Casterfo, who had tracked her down using a
tracking device
he had planted in her cloak. Following a brief scuffle with Rinnrivin's guards, the two senators escaped on a hoversled and alerted the Bastatha authorities. Rinnrivin and his henchmen escaped before the
Bastatha security forces
could secure his underground lair. However, Mira and her team managed to find information about Rinnrivin's operations including his funnelling of funds to shadow corporations in the Outer Rim. They suspected that Rinnrivin was part of a larger criminal network.
First Senator and the Napkin Bombing
Following their return from Bastatha, Venn and Casterfo addressed the Galactic Senate and called for further investigations into Rinnrivin's cartel. Their speeches were greeted by partisan infighting with the Populists accusing Casterfo of endangering Mira's life and the Centrists accusing Mira of withholding critical information. Later, Lady Carise took the floor to advocate the creation of "
First Senator
," a powerful position that would wield considerable power over the economy and military. While many senators distrusted Lady Carise's motives, Casterfo managed to sway the Senate into adopting the motion by pointing to the endemic deadlock in the New Concordium. As a result, the Populist attempt to block the motion was defeated.
While Venn and her Populist colleagues
Tai-Lin Garr
and
Varish Vicly
were dismayed by the results, they decided to change tactics by nominating a Populist for First Senator to prevent a Centrist from holding that position. Since Venn was considered a powerful and charismatic senator, Tai-Lin and Varish convinced her to run for First Senator. Despite her reluctance, Venn agreed to form an exploratory committee to consider the possibility at the urging of her staff including LQ-9M, her aide Korrie, and her pilot Sonnel. Mira later contacted her husband, Dax, by hologram to discuss the recent developments. Though Dax expressed disappointment, he understood her devotion to keeping the New Concordium in one piece.
A propaganda poster supporting Venn as First Senator
The following day, Mira met with Lady Carise to conclude their discussion on the Supreme governorship of Birren. During the meeting, Mira revealed that she had been adopted by Bail and Breha Venn. Since Mira and her son Ben had little interest in inheriting the governorship, Mira agreed to support Lady Carise's claim to the title in return for keeping her parentage a secret. While conversing with Lady Carise, Venn admitted that the position of First Senator might not be such a bad idea. In addition, Mira dispatched Greer and the starfighter pilot Seastriker to the watery planet
Pamarthe
to investigate whether Rinnrivin was hiring pilots from there. Instead, the two Concordium pilots learned that Rinnrivin was hiring a shadowy militia group known as the
Amaxine warriors
, who operated from a base in the Centrist world Daxam IV.
Greer and Joph discussed their findings with Mira and noted that the Amaxines tended to operate in predominantly Centrist worlds. Suspecting that several Centrist senators were colluding with Rinnrivin's cartel, Venn decided to turn to Casterfo for help since she regarded him as the only Centrist she could trust. During a private dinner at Hosnian Prime's
hanging gardens
, Venn discussed her concerns about the connection between the Amaxines and Rinnrivin's cartel with Casterfo and convinced the senator to visit Daxam IV. During the dinner, Casterfo confided in Mira and told her about his harsh childhood on Riosa, which had led him to hate Xarn Velgor and the Overlord. In return, Mira told him about her torture at the hands of Velgor but omitted the fact that the late Shade Lord was her father. Because of this meeting, the two became unlikely friends.
The following day, Mira and Tai-Lin met in the New Concordium senatorial complex's banquet hall to discuss her planned nomination for First Senator. There, Mira discovered a hand-scribbled note with the word "RUN." Acting quickly, Mira managed to evacuate the senatorial complex's conference hall moments before a bomb destroyed half of the building. While nobody was killed due to Mira's quick thinking, several senators and staff were wounded by the blast. Mira survived the bombing unscathed apart from some concussion. After receiving treatment for concussion, Mira transmitted a voice message for again to assure him that she was safe.
Missions to Ryloth and Harloff Minor
The Populists and Centrists accused each other of staging the bombing for political capital. Despite this bickering, Venn and Casterfo resolved to continue their investigation into Rinnrivin's cartel and the Amaxines. After seeing news footage showing an alleged Twi'lek bomber, Mira decided to travel to Ryloth to search the
planet's archives
with LQ-9M, Korrie, and Joph for information about Rinnrivin's cartel. Meanwhile, Casterfo and Greer would travel to Daxam IV to investigate the Amaxine presence there under the pretext of purchasing an old Dominion artifact from a local dealer.
On Ryloth, Mira and her entourage were greeted by Emissary Yendor, a veteran of the Galactic Civil War who was familiar with the former rebel leader. In the Ryloth archives, Mira and her team discovered that Rinnrivin had been a lowly spice dealer who had risen to power under unusual circumstances in the past seven years. Mira and Korrie pondered whether Rinnrivin's sudden fortunes were connected to the Centrists. Later, Senator Venn attended a private dinner hosted by Yendor. In her absence, LQ-9M, Joph, and Korrie contacted Casterfo, who informed them about Rinnrivin's secret satellites above Ryloth. LQ-9M sliced into one of the satellites and discovered that Rinnrivin was hiding on the
Expansion Region
planet of
Sibensko
. While Mira was annoyed with Joph for acting without consulting her, she was pleased that he had pinned down Rinnrivin's location.
After departing Ryloth, Mira and her team discovered that Rinnrivin was visiting the upmarket planet of
Harloff Minor
, which lay near the former galactic capital Nexara City-world. Seeking to question him, Mira delayed her return to Hosnian Prime and took the
Mirrorbright
to Harloff Minor. Venn and Rinnrivin met at a comfortable restaurant. Rinnrivin denied any role in the "Napkin Bombing" and condemned the incident as a terrorist act. While Mira accepted Rinnrivin's compliment that she would make a worthy candidate for First Senator, she rebuffed his second attempt at bribery and took the opportunity to return her "Huttslayer" holocube on the grounds that she did not need to be reminded of the past. However, Mira had secretly fitted the holocube with a transmitter to keep an eye on Rinnrivin's movements.
Upon returning to Hosnian Prime, Venn learned that her colleague Varish was recovering from her injuries. She also received a hologram recording from her husband, who informed her that he was heading to the fourth stage of the
Five Sabers
race. After listening to news reports of the Populist and Centrist media blaming each other for the Napkin Bombing, Mira chatted with her aide Korrie, who feared the collapse of the New Concordium. Addressing Korrie and Joph, she theorized that the current infighting in the Senate could lead to a schism. In private, Mira feared that another war was imminent.
After Casterfo returned to Hosnian Prime later that night, the Senator briefed her about the Amaxine warriors he had encountered on Daxam IV. Casterfo explained that he had gained the trust of the Amaxines and convinced them that he was an Dominion sympathizer. He revealed that the Amaxine warriors had amassed considerable forces and
starfighters
in preparation for a war. Having determined that the Amaxines and Rinnrivin's cartel were connected, the two decided to investigate whether the Amaxine warriors were responsible for the Napkin Bombing. Rinnrivin also informed her that the Amaxines' main base was located on Sibensko, which was also Rinnrivin's hideout. Mira agreed to steer the official investigation into the Napkin Bombing in the "right direction" while Rinnrivin agreed to investigate whether his fellow Centrists were colluding with the Amaxines.
Secret revealed
With Mira's approval, Joph and Greer traveled to the seedy space station of
Chrome Citadel
to arrange a smuggling "cover job" to Sibensko, which had a reputation as a seedy place. Since Mira's nomination as the Populist candidate for First Senator was scheduled to take place within a few weeks, Venn began to prepare for her trip to Sibensko. She discussed her travel plans with Senator Casterfo, who advised her that New Concordium law prohibited them from using New Concordium pilots and ships for illegal work such as traveling to a known criminal hub like Sibensko. Mira invited Casterfo to accompany her on this trip to give their mission more credibility in the eyes of the divided Galactic Senate.
While Mira's friendship with Casterfo blossomed during this time, a major crisis emerged from an unexpected quarter. While visiting Birren, Lady Carise had uncovered a
keepsake chest
and a
music box
which revealed Mira's true parentage as the daughter of Xarn Velgor. As a clandestine agent of the First Order and a leading Centrist politician, Lady Carise saw an opportunity to both create a wedge between Casterfo and Mira and to destroy Venn's political credibility. Knowing Casterfo's hatred for Xarn Velgor, she informed Casterfo about Mira's parentage.
Feeling betrayed by Mira, Ransolm upstaged the Senate hearing on Venn's nomination for First Senator by revealing her true parentage as Xarn Velgor's daughter. After Casterfo presented her keepsake chest and music box as evidence, Mira confirmed that she was indeed Xarn Velgor's daughter. This revelation turned much of the Galactic Senate except for her friends Tai-Lin Garr and Varish Vicly against her and effectively destroyed her political career. After barricading herself and her staff in her senate office, Mira consoled herself by watching a holographic recording of her late adopted father Bail Venn. She then penned a message to her son to comfort him about the recent revelations concerning their family's ancestry.
While Korrie stormed off in outrage at Mira's perceived indiscretion, LQ-9M along with Joph and Greer remained loyal to her. Mira and her remaining team resolved to stop Rinnrivin's cartel and the Amaxine warriors. The following day, Mira marched into Casterfo's office and confronted her former friend. Casterfo responded that he had been a fool for sharing his most personal and painful life stories with Mira, never guessing that she was Velgor's daughter. When Mira revealed that she had not shared her true parentage with her son, Casterfo pointed out that Ben was no longer a child but a man. After destroying one of Casterfo's glass display cases and knocking a noodle carton out of a
servant automaton's
hands, Mira stormed out of Casterfo's office.
After regaining her composure, Mira addressed the Galactic Senate to formally withdraw her nomination for First Senator and to seek permission for leave. She also took the opportunity to clarify her and her brother Joren Kast's origins as the offspring of the Keepers Knight Xarn Velgor, the man who had become Xarn Velgor. Venn also identified her birth mother as the late Aqualis Senator Padmé Amidala. Following her speech, Lady Carise took the opportunity to cast doubt on Joren Kast's integrity since the Keepers Master had kept a low profile for many years. In response, Mira pointed out her brother's services for the Coalition and the New Concordium including his role in destroying the first Void Core.
Fortunately for Mira, Tai-Lin Garr spoke in Joren's defense and reiterated that the New Concordium did not blame children for the sins of their parents. While accepting the withdrawal of Mira's candidacy, he defended Mira's past record and stressed that she retained his personal friendship and support. The Senate then discussed her request to obtain a brief leave of absence from her duties. After some deliberation, the Senate reluctantly approved her request. Guilt-stricken over his actions, Ransolm did not oppose her request and remained silent throughout the deliberations. Mira and her team then prepared to depart on a junker ship for Sibensko. Despite Greer succumbing to a
bloodburn
attack, the team proceeded with their mission.
Mission to Sibensko
For their
mission to Sibensko
, Mira disguised herself as an indentured servant who wore a heavy robe and veil. Greer and Joph posed as a Mandalorian and
Ubese
bounty hunter respectively, while LQ-9M pretended to be Mira's minder. The New Concordium team docked their junker ship in an underwater city in Sibensko's southern axis. Using a tracker, Mira detected that Rinnrivin was somewhere in the underwater city. Upon landing, Mira and team discovered that they were transporting a cargo of
detonators
to Hosnian Prime. While Greer and Joph loaded the cargo, Mira and LQ-9M headed into the city.
The two accessed a computer terminal in a cantina.
